### [102] [EmoWorld: A Decoupled Affective Field for Controllable Emotional Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06231)
*Bingyuan Wang,Baistan Zhyldyzbekov,Kunyu Feng,Zeyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EmoWorld提出一种在冻结的流匹配视频扩散Transformer（Video DiT）中解耦全局氛围、情感语义线索和时间进程的视频情感生成框架，通过一次性的方向提取与可复用线索库构建，结合三种情感引导策略（VAS、SAS、TAS），在不更新生成器参数的前提下显著提升目标情感对齐度并改善时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器将全局氛围、影响情感的中层语义线索和时间进展捆绑在单一文本条件中，导致情感表达模糊且难以精确控制；作者希望将这三个因素解耦，从而实现对视频情感的独立、细粒度调控。

Method: 在冻结的流匹配视频扩散Transformer框架上，通过一次性准备阶段从几何保持的中性全景图和情感编辑全景图中提取逐层情感方向和可复用线索库；推理时采用三种策略：VAS（视觉氛围引导）将氛围方向注入隐藏状态，SAS（语义情感引导）为语义线索分离出可独立缩放的低秩提示残差，TAS（时间情感引导）在去噪步和时间轴上对端点残差场进行插值。

Result: 在Wan2.2基准上：VAS将目标情感对齐度提升19%，同时将时间波动代理指标降低48%；SAS将目标情感对齐度提升37%并将检测到的情感线索提升36%；TAS将过渡单调性比最强基线提升15%。在27种情感类别的文生视频和图生视频设置中进行了评估，支持跨多种Video-DiT骨干网络迁移，并支持相机条件组合而无须更新生成器参数。

Conclusion: EmoWorld证明在冻结的视频扩散Transformer中解耦情感氛围、语义线索和时间进程是可行且有效的，实现了可移植、参数高效的情感可控视频生成，为多维度情感细粒度控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Emotion shapes how viewers interpret a scene, yet existing video generators entangle global atmosphere, affect-bearing semantic cues, and temporal progression within a single text condition. We present EmoWorld, a framework that decouples these factors within a frozen flow-matching video diffusion transformer (Video DiT). A one-time preparation stage extracts layer-specific affect directions and a reusable cue library from geometry-preserving neutral and emotion-edited panoramas. At inference, Visual Atmosphere Steering (VAS) injects atmosphere directions into hidden states, Semantic Affective Steering (SAS) isolates a separately scalable prompt residual for semantic cues, and Temporal Affective Steering (TAS) interpolates endpoint residual fields across denoising and video time. On Wan2.2, VAS improves target-emotion alignment by 19% while reducing a temporal-fluctuation proxy by 48%; SAS improves target-emotion alignment by 37% and increases detected affect-bearing cues by 36%; and TAS improves transition monotonicity by 15% over the strongest baseline. EmoWorld is evaluated across 27 emotion categories in text-to-video and image-to-video settings, demonstrates portability across multiple Video-DiT backbones, and supports camera-conditioned composition without updating generator parameters.

</details>


### [103] [Depth-Guided Video Object Counting in Crowded Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06236)
*Yuanjing Xu,Xinyan Liu,Weidong Chen,Zixuan Zou,Linhao Zhang,Zhuangzhe Meng,Antoni B. Chan,Weigang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出深度引导检测器（DG-Det）框架，通过融合RGB与深度信息（多尺度RGB-D交叉注意力）和显式遮挡预测来提升拥挤遮挡场景下的视频目标计数性能，并引入统一的去重框架消除跨帧冗余计数，同时发布新的RGB-D视频目标计数数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频目标计数方法仅依赖RGB信息，在拥挤和遮挡条件下判别能力有限，难以准确区分和计数密集场景中的目标实例。

Method: 1) 提出DG-Det（Depth-Guided Detector），集成深度线索与多尺度RGB-D交叉注意力，增强空间理解；2) 引入显式遮挡预测模块提升遮挡场景检测鲁棒性；3) 设计通用后处理流程，包含统一的去重框架以消除跨帧冗余计数。

Result: 在基线方法基础上MAE降低62.01%，RMSE也获得一致性改善；同时发布了包含深度信息和多目标类别的RGB-D视频目标计数数据集。

Conclusion: 通过引入深度信息与显式遮挡建模，可显著提升拥挤场景下视频目标计数的准确性和鲁棒性，且开源了代码与数据集以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Our primary objective is to advance video object counting in crowded scenes, aiming to robustly count all instances of a target category based on given text or visual prompts. Existing methods rely on RGB information, limiting their discriminative ability in crowded and occluded conditions. To address this, we propose a Depth-Guided Detector (DG-Det) along with a general post-processing pipeline. By integrating depth cues with multi-scale RGB-D cross-attention and explicit occlusion prediction, our method enhances spatial understanding and achieves robust detection in crowded and occluded scenes. Furthermore, we introduce a unified de-duplication framework to eliminate cross-frame redundant counting. To facilitate future research, we also release a new RGB-D Video Object Counting dataset featuring depth information and multiple object categories persequence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a 62.01\% reduction in MAE compared to existing baselines, and also produces consistent improvements in RMSE. We provide the source code at https://github.com/streamer-AP/DG-Net and the dataset at https://huggingface.co/datasets/aerospace123/RGBD-VideoCount.

</details>


### [104] [PRISM: Distribution-Gated Flow Matching for Controllable Unpaired Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06240)
*Elad Yoshai,Natan T. Shaked*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM是一个无GAN的flow-matching框架，通过引入学习得到的逐特征门控（per-feature gate）替代传统的全局噪声/引导控制，实现非成对图像到图像转换中"保留什么、改变什么"的精细分离。门控由源特征到目标特征分布的标准化距离推导而来，同时控制初始化混合和ODE积分时序，并支持推理时基于文本或检测器的局部覆盖。在五个自然及生物医学基准上取得最好的FID/KID结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的非成对图像翻译方法通常使用全局噪声或引导值来控制保存程度，无法在同一张图像中有效区分需要保留的内容与需要改变的外观，导致结构保存与目标真实感之间的平衡难以精细控制。

Method: PRISM采用flow-matching框架（无GAN），核心创新是学习一个逐特征门控：其空间先验来自每个源特征相对于目标特征分布的标准化距离（远则放开、一致则保留）。该门控同时控制两个环节：一是初始化阶段将真实源潜码与任务匹配的噪声/扰动混合，二是ODE积分过程中的运输时序。扰动与任务匹配，结构保持型翻译用内容锚定的AdaIN，结构改变型翻译用部分锚定。门控可在推理时被文本或检测器的局部覆盖机制所修改，无需重新训练。

Result: 在五个基准（AFHQ猫→狗、CelebA-HQ外观翻译、昼夜重光照、虚拟染色、乳腺冰冻→永久组织病理）的共享同划分协议评估下，PRISM在四个基准上取得最优Inception FID和KID，在第五个上结果具有竞争力；在组织病理学上，其细胞核计数比最接近理想值，实现了目标真实感与结构保留之间有利的平衡。

Conclusion: PRISM通过逐特征门控替代全局控制，有效解决了非成对翻译中内容保留与外观改变的冲突问题，同时支持灵活的推理时局部控制，在自然图像和生物医学图像两个领域均展现出优越性能，为无配对图像翻译中结构调整提供了新技术范式。

Abstract: Unpaired image-to-image translation must decide, per image, what to change and what to preserve without paired supervision. Many diffusion-based unpaired translators control preservation through a single global noise or guidance value applied across the image, which cannot separate content to keep from appearance to change. We present PRISM, a GAN-free flow-matching framework that replaces this global control with a learned per-feature gate. The gate's spatial prior is derived from each source feature's standardized distance to the target feature distribution, so features far from the target are freed while target-consistent features are preserved. The same gate controls both the initialization, which mixes the real source latent with a task-matched corruption, and the transport timing during Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) integration. The corruption is matched to the task, content-anchored (AdaIN) for structure-preserving translation and partially anchored for structure-changing translation, and the gate can be overridden locally at inference from text or a detector without retraining, preserving important structures of the original image while still generating realistic results. We evaluate PRISM on five natural and biomedical benchmarks (AFHQ cat->dog, CelebA-HQ appearance translation, day->night relighting, virtual staining, and breast frozen->permanent histopathology). Among the evaluated methods under a shared same-split protocol, PRISM attains the best Inception FID and KID on four benchmarks and a competitive result on the fifth, and on histopathology yields the nuclei-count ratio closest to the ideal, supporting a favorable balance between target realism and structural preservation.

</details>


### [105] [Toward Deployable Bangla Sign Language Recognition with Expert-Validated Data and a Lightweight Attention-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06252)
*Saad Ahmed,Md Khalid Syfullaha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了RSBdSL38数据集（覆盖孟加拉手语全部38个手型，包含10,874张经专家验证的图像），并设计了仅29.8万参数的轻量级注意力卷积网络。该网络从零训练即可达到96.37%准确率，与ImageNet预训练大模型差距在1.08个百分点以内，但参数量减少8.5-68倍、计算量减少1.3-21.7倍。量化后仅0.48MB，可在普通智能手机上以3.98ms/张的速度运行，实现了终端设备可部署的手语识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉听障人群主要用孟加拉手语（BdSL）交流，自动识别可扩展教育和服务的可及性。但现有系统存在两个关键问题：一是使用实验室受控环境、未经专家验证的数据集；二是依赖重量级预训练骨干网络，参数量和计算量过大，无法在个人设备（如智能手机）上部署。因此需要同时解决数据质量与模型轻量化两大挑战。

Method: 1) 数据层面：构建RSBdSL38数据集，从孟加拉三所特殊教育学校采集真实手语者的10,874张图像，覆盖全部38种BdSL手型（对应51个孟加拉字母），并由专家验证。2) 模型层面：提出轻量级注意力卷积网络（29.8万参数），包含分组瓶颈残差块、通道与空间注意力、多尺度深度可分离手部特征块、双池化及Swish激活函数。3) 评估层面：从零训练，对比9种ImageNet预训练高效架构；在多数据集上测试迁移性能、消融实验验证各组件贡献，并通过Grad-CAM可解释性分析确认模型关注手部区域；采用分签名者独立划分评估泛化能力；最后进行量化部署测试。

Result: 从零训练达到96.37%准确率（五种子平均95.72%±0.54%）；在六个公开BdSL基准上达到92.95-98.33%，合并语料97.04%，BdSL-38零样本76.25%。移除任一架构组件损失7.61-89.30个点，远大于训练策略的影响（≤3.17）。留出6/36签名者的跨人划分为85.18%。量化至0.48MB后，在15.5MB内存占用的普通智能手机上以3.98ms/张运行。参数量较预训练架构减少8.5-68倍，MACs减少1.3-21.7倍。

Conclusion: RSBdSL38数据集与提出的轻量级从零训练模型共同将基准准确率转化为可部署的无障碍工具，以预训练骨干模型的一小部分成本实现接近甚至匹配的精度。该工作填补了孟加拉手语识别在数据质量与终端部署两方面的空白。

Abstract: Deaf and hard-of-hearing people in Bangladesh communicate mainly through Bangla Sign Language (BdSL). Automatic BdSL recognition on personal devices could widen access to education and services. Existing systems use controlled-setting datasets without expert verification and heavyweight pretrained backbones unsuited to on-device use. We introduce RSBdSL38, 10,874 expert-validated images spanning all 38 BdSL hand signs, representing the 51 letters of the Bangla alphabet, recorded from real signers at three special-needs schools across Bangladesh. We propose a lightweight attention based convolutional network of 298,470 parameters, built from grouped bottleneck residual blocks, channel and spatial attention, a multi-scale depthwise hand-feature block, dual pooling, and Swish activations. Trained from scratch, it attains 96.37% accuracy (95.72% +- 0.54% over five seeds), within 1.08 percentage points of the best of nine ImageNet-pretrained efficient architectures under an identical protocol, using 8.5 to 68x fewer parameters and 1.3 to 21.7x fewer MACs. Retrained, it reaches 92.95 to 98.33% on six public BdSL benchmarks, 97.04% on a merged corpus, and 76.25% zero-shot on BdSL-38. Removing any architectural stage costs 7.61 to 89.30 points, against at most 3.17 for the training recipe. Grad-CAM with deletion-insertion and weight-randomization checks confirms that predictions follow the signing hand. A signer-independent split holding out 6 of 36 signers yields 85.18%. Quantized to 0.48 MB, it runs at 3.98 ms per image within a 15.5 MB footprint on a commodity smartphone. Together, RSBdSL38 and our from-scratch model turn benchmark accuracy into deployable accessibility at a fraction of pretrained-backbone cost; dataset, code, and models are released.

</details>


### [106] [HoloCount: A Holistic Visual Counting Benchmark for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06420)
*Jinhong Deng,Limeng Qiao,Guanglu Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HoloCount，一个基于三层等级分类法的综合视觉计数诊断基准，用于系统性评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在语义计数、分析计数与鲁棒性测试三个层面的计数能力。通过对20多个先进MLLMs的全面评测，揭示了模型从感知到复杂推理任务中的显著性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在定性场景理解上表现优异，但定量计数的精度仍是瓶颈，存在持续性数值幻觉问题。已有的计数基准主要关注简化场景下的基础感知，无法捕捉逻辑约束或对抗条件下出现的复杂失败模式，缺乏一个能够系统诊断模型计数能力的综合基准。

Method: 构建HoloCount基准，围绕三层等级分类法设计：(1)语义计数，评估原子级与基于属性的枚举；(2)分析计数，通过空间和集合推理评估逻辑组合能力；(3)鲁棒性测试，针对高密度场景和语言偏差等恶劣条件及接地反先验探测模型完整性。对20多个SOTA MLLMs进行系统评测。

Result: 评测结果显示存在关键性能差距：即便是顶级模型，当任务从感知层面向复杂分析推理和对抗场景过渡时，性能也显著下降，揭示了当前MLLMs在定量计数上的系统性局限。

Conclusion: HoloCount提供了当前MLLM计数能力的系统性全景图，为开发更可靠、更接地气的多模态系统提供了路线图，有助于推动多模态智能在精细粒度定位与空间推理方面的进步。

Abstract: Visual counting is a fundamental pillar of multimodal intelligence, requiring a seamless integration of fine-grained grounding and spatial reasoning. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in qualitative scene understanding, their quantitative precision remains a significant bottleneck, often characterized by persistent numerical hallucinations. Existing counting benchmarks primarily focus on basic perception in simplified contexts, failing to capture the complex failure modes that emerge under logical constraints or adversarial conditions. To address these limitations, we introduce HoloCount, a holistic and diagnostically rich benchmark structured around a three-level hierarchical taxonomy. HoloCount evaluates MLLMs across: (1) Semantic Counting, focusing on atomic and property-based enumeration; (2) Analytical Counting, assessing logical composition through spatial and set-based reasoning; and (3) Robustness Testing, probing model integrity against adverse scenarios and grounded counter-priors, such as high-density scenes and linguistic biases. Through an exhaustive evaluation of over 20 state-of-the-art MLLMs, we reveal a critical performance gap: even top-tier models degrade significantly as tasks transition from perception to complex analytical reasoning and adverse scenarios. Our findings provide a systematic landscape of current MLLM counting capabilities and offer a roadmap for developing more grounded and reliable multimodal systems. The dataset is available at https://mm-mvr.github.io/HoloCount/.

</details>


### [107] [MASS: Multiplayer World Models with Authoritative Shared State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06257)
*Ziqi Cai,Siqi Yang,Yimu Wang,Zixian Gao,Yunheng Liu,Shuchen Weng,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAS框架，通过学习型权威共享状态将世界动力学与视角渲染解耦，实现多人在线环境中的可扩展、一致性的世界模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在多玩家环境中将世界状态与视角相关的视觉潜在表示纠缠在一起，导致计算冗余、视角不一致和可扩展性差。

Method: 受多人游戏架构启发，MAS包含两个学习模块：一个Logic Engine从联合动作推进全局权威的类型化共享状态（无需手写转移函数，作为唯一循环记忆和同步参考）；一个Rendering Engine从共享状态按需生成任意相机的独立且一致的视角。

Result: 在多人Snake基准上，MAS相比最先进的多视角基线在状态准确性和跨视角一致性上表现更优，并能将1,024个并发玩家推进10,000个循环步。

Conclusion: 显式的权威状态建模为可扩展且一致的多智能体世界模拟提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Current video world models struggle in multiplayer environments because they entangle world state with view-dependent visual latents, leading to redundant compute, view inconsistencies, and poor scalability. We propose MAS (Multiplayer world models with Authoritative Shared State) to resolve this limitation. Inspired by multiplayer game architectures, MAS disentangles world dynamics and view rendering. A learned Logic Engine advances a global, authoritative typed state from joint actions without any hand-written transition function, acting as the sole recurrent memory and synchronization reference. From this shared state, a learned Rendering Engine generates independent and consistent views for any requested camera on demand. This explicit disentangling allows MAS to achieve superior state accuracy and lower cross-view inconsistency compared to state-of-the-art multi-view baselines on a matched multiplayer Snake benchmark. It advances predicted worlds with 1,024 concurrent players for 10,000 recurrent steps. Our results show that explicit, authoritative state modeling provides a practical foundation for scalable and consistent multi-agent world simulation.

</details>


### [108] [UQ-Loc: Uncertainty-Aware LiDAR Scene Coordinate Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06307)
*Jacek Komorowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UQ-Loc，一种基于LiDAR的场景坐标回归方法，通过各向异性高斯协方差头预测每体素的全3x3正定协方差矩阵，并在推理时采用基于不确定性加权的SC2-PCR求解器和马氏距离内点检验，实现更准确的6自由度定位与良好校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LiDAR的场景坐标回归方法仅产生确定性预测，丢弃了能提升鲁棒性和下游决策的偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty）信息。

Method: 扩展LightLoc架构，新增各向异性高斯协方差头预测每个体素的全3x3正定协方差矩阵；训练采用负对数似然损失并加入基于kNN的空间平滑正则化；推理使用改进的SC2-PCR求解器，包含不确定性加权种子评分和马氏距离内点检验；评估采用期望校准误差（ECE）作为不确定性质量指标。

Result: 实验表明UQ-Loc在6自由度定位精度上取得一致的改进，同时产生良好校准的协方差预测。

Conclusion: 将偶然不确定性与场景坐标回归结合，不仅提升了定位精度，还提供了可靠的不确定性度量，有助于增强系统的鲁棒性和下游决策可靠性。

Abstract: LiDAR-based Scene Coordinate Regression (SCR) maps point clouds directly to 3D scene coordinates, enabling precise 6-DoF localisation without explicit map retrieval. However, existing methods produce deterministic predictions, discarding aleatoric uncertainty that could improve robustness and downstream decision-making. We present UQ-Loc, which extends the LightLoc architecture with an anisotropic Gaussian covariance head that predicts a full 3x3 positive-definite covariance matrix per voxel. Training uses a Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) loss augmented with a kNN-based spatial smoothness regulariser, while inference employs a modified SC2-PCR solver with uncertainty-weighted seed scoring and a Mahalanobis-distance inlier test. We adopt Expected Calibration Error (ECE) as a principled metric for evaluating the quality of the predicted uncertainty. Experiments demonstrate that UQ-Loc achieves consistent improvement in 6-DoF localization accuracy while producing well-calibrated covariances.

</details>


### [109] [Does FLAIR super-resolution erase or hallucinate small white-matter lesions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06311)
*Zahra Khodakarami,Yue Li,Pulkit Khandelwal,John Detre,Sandhitsu Das,Christopher Brown,David Wolk,Paul Yushkevich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究调查了在WMH分割前应用超分辨率重建的影响，发现SR的主要效应是擦除小的真实病灶而非幻觉生成病灶，且擦除率随层厚增加而升高；尽管所有重建方法都比原始厚层图像有所改进，但ECLARE在恢复小病灶信号方面表现最佳，而多对比INR并不优于三次插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床FLAIR图像通常以厚层采集，面内分辨率差，超分辨率被广泛用于恢复各向同性体积。然而，将SR应用于WMH分割之前是否保留病灶内容尚不清楚——模型可能擦除小的真实病灶或幻觉出不存在的病灶，这直接影响WMH作为脑血管病理和神经退行性标志物的可靠测量。

Method: 使用ADNI队列中29名个体的1mm各向同性高分辨率FLAIR扫描，由专家手动分割WMH，并将其降质为模拟的3mm和5mm穿面采集。采用多对比隐式神经表示（INR）、单对比自监督模型（ECLARE）和三次插值三种方法进行超分辨重建。在四种WMH分割方法中选择对HR小病灶最敏感的MARS-WMH进行评估，使用检测灵敏度、擦除率和幻觉率作为指标。原始HR FLAIR和模拟厚层分别作为性能上限和下限基准。

Result: SR的主要效应是擦除小的真实病灶而非幻觉生成病灶，且随切片厚度增加而加剧；但每种重建方法仍比原始厚层图像的病灶检测有所改善。ECLARE在两种厚度下均最好地恢复小病灶信号，INR方法不优于三次插值。

Conclusion: 将超分辨率应用于WMH分割前，会导致小病灶被擦除的风险，且厚度越大风险越高；自监督的ECLARE方法对病灶保留最有保障，而当前流行的INR方法在病灶保留方面并无优势，临床应用中应谨慎选择SR策略。

Abstract: White matter hyperintensities (WMH), bright regions on Fluid-attenuated Inversion Recovery (FLAIR) scans are associated with cerebrovascular pathology and neurodegeneration. FLAIR is usually acquired with thick slices in clinical settings, giving it poor through-plane resolution. Super-resolution (SR) is a widely used method for recovering an isotropic volume from an anisotropic scan. Yet whether applying it prior to WMH segmentation preserves lesion content remains unknown: a model may erase small real lesions or hallucinate absent ones. We used 1-mm isotropic high-resolution (HR) FLAIR scans from 29 individuals in the ADNI cohort, each manually segmented for WMH by an expert. Then, we degraded each to simulated 3 and 5 mm through-plane acquisitions. Multi-contrast implicit neural representation (INR), a single-contrast self-supervised model (ECLARE), and cubic interpolation were used to upsample them onto the HR grid. WMH segmentation from a simulated thick slice and the original HR FLAIR set the floor and ceiling, respectively, for the per-lesion analysis. Of four WMH segmentation methods (WMH-SynthSeg, segcsvd, MARS-WMH, TrUE-Net), we ran the analysis under the most sensitive one to small lesions on HR (MARS-WMH) with the evaluation metrics of detection sensitivity, erasure rate (HR-detected lesions lost after reconstruction), and hallucination rate (predicted components absent from both the manual and HR segmentation). The dominant effect of SR was erasure of small real lesions, not hallucination, and it increased with slice thickness, though every reconstruction still improved lesion detection over the raw thick slice. ECLARE recovered small lesion signal best at both thicknesses, while the INR was no better than cubic interpolation.

</details>

### [110] [A Foundational EDM2-Based Generative Model for High-Resolution Synthetic Fetal Ultrasound Imaging from Open Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05471)
*Harvey Mannering,Yilin Zhang,Ziao Liu,Zhiwu Huang,Jacqueline Matthew,Miguel Xochicale*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于EDM2扩散架构的高分辨率胎儿超声图像合成框架，在多个公开数据集上训练生成512x512六类解剖图像。生成图像在FID和下游分类任务上表现优异，集成分类准确率达93.36%超过纯真实数据训练；但临床专家评估显示合成图像真实感评分（2.67/5）低于真实图像，存在平滑伪影、斑点不规则和解剖不一致等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产前超声成像对胎儿健康评估至关重要，但AI在该领域的发展受到数据稀缺、隐私限制和标注困难的三重制约。现有数据集难以支撑深度学习模型的训练，需要一种能够生成高质量合成胎儿超声图像的方法来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 基于EDM2扩散模型架构，在多个公开数据集上训练高分辨率（512x512）胎儿超声图像生成框架，覆盖六个解剖类别。通过扩散模型生成合成图像，并在下游胎儿平面分类任务上进行微调验证，同时进行临床专家主观评估。

Result: 生成图像在FID得分上表现更优（图像质量提升）；微调后的集成分类准确率达到93.36%，超过仅用真实数据训练的结果；但临床专家对100张图像的平均真实感评分为2.67/5，真实图像评分高于合成图像，主要伪影包括平滑处理过度、斑点不规则和解剖结构不一致。

Conclusion: 扩散模型生成的胎儿超声图像在客观指标和下游任务中具有实用价值，可作为数据增强手段提升分类性能；但当前合成图像与真实图像在临床视觉真实性上仍存在差距，需进一步改进以应对临床严格应用场景。

Abstract: Prenatal ultrasound imaging is key for assessing fetal health, but AI progress is limited by scarce, privacy-restricted, and hard-to-annotate datasets. We propose a high-resolution fetal ultrasound synthesis framework based on the EDM2 diffusion architecture, trained on multiple public datasets to generate 512x512 images across six anatomical classes. Our method achieved improved image quality with lower FID scores and enhanced downstream fetal plane classification, reaching 93.36% ensemble accuracy after fine-tuning, surpassing real-data-only training. Clinical evaluation by an experienced fetal ultrasound specialist (10+ years) on 100 images yielded a mean realism score of 2.67/5, with real images rated higher than synthetic. Artefacts included smoothing, speckle irregularities, and anatomical inconsistencies. Code, data, models and other resources to reproduce this work are available at https://github.com/xfetus/fetal-ultrasound-edm2.

</details>


### [111] [Hyperspectral Calibration Detection: A Novel Concept For Change Detection With Unsupervised Incremental Safe Pseudo-Labeling Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06028)
*Chia-Hsiang Lin,Shih-Min Hsu,Ching-Yun Liang,Jocelyn Chanussot,Jhih-Yan Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全无监督的高光谱变化检测（HCD）算法HyperLUCID，通过迭代增强训练集和光谱校准函数来补偿双时相图像的采集条件差异，实现零标签标注的机载边缘计算检测任务，在多个真实基准数据集上取得约93.6%-97.9%的总体精度，且比大多数基准方法快1-2个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流HCD算法多为半监督方法，即使能达到很低的样本标注率，但在某些实际场景（如机载边缘计算中需要即时检测响应）中，新获取的图像无法进行地面真值标注，因此需要实现零标注要求的全无监督HCD方法。

Method: 提出HyperLUCID算法，核心思想包括：(1) 通过迭代增强的训练集安全收集未变化的像素样本；(2) 学习迭代精化的光谱校准函数以补偿双时相图像中采集条件的变异性；(3) 通过分析校准后的光谱使变化像素易于检测；(4) 结合轻量级模型，适用于机载检测任务。

Result: HyperLUCID在多个真实基准HCD数据集上取得了最先进的性能（约93.6%-97.9%总体精度），同时计算效率极高，比大多数基准HCD方法快约1-2个数量级。

Conclusion: 提出的全无监督HyperLUCID算法成功实现了零标注要求下的高效高精度高光谱变化检测，特别适合机载边缘计算等即时检测场景，代码已开源。

Abstract: Hyperspectral change detection (HCD) has found numerous key applications, such as land cover monitoring. The majority of benchmark HCD algorithms are semi-supervised methods, and some of them can even achieve very low sample labeling rates. However, in some practical scenarios, such as those requiring immediate detection responses for onboard edge computing, we need to achieve the zero-label requirement as ground-truth labeling would not be available onboard for newly acquired images. In this work, we propose a fully unsupervised HCD algorithm, together with a lightweight model, quite suitable for onboard detection missions. Based on an iteratively augmented training set that safely collects some unchanged pixel samples, we learn an iteratively refined spectrum calibration function that eventually compensates the variability of acquisition conditions (often observed in bitemporal images), thereby making the changed pixels easily detectable by analyzing the calibrated spectra. The proposed hyperspectral looping unsupervised calibration and incremental detection (HyperLUCID) algorithm is not only computationally efficient (around 1 to 2 orders of magnitude faster than most benchmark HCD methods), but has also achieved state-of-the-art results (around 93.6% to 97.9% overall accuracy) on several real benchmark HCD datasets. Source codes: https://github.com/IHCLab/HyperLUCID.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [VLAff: Vision-Language-Affordance Model for Unified Actionable Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05215)
*Jihoon Oh,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLAff框架，从第一人称人类视频中利用3D重建技术提取物体中心的可操作affordance（视觉、抓取、轨迹），构建包含204K片段、5.6M视觉affordance和11.6M抓取/轨迹affordance的大规模数据集EgoAffordance，并训练一个基于大型视觉语言模型的统一基础模型，能够同时预测视觉affordance热图、抓取姿态和运动轨迹，实现零样本机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类视频中学习操作技能是扩展机器人学习的有前景方向，但人类与机器人之间的embodiment差异（身体结构不匹配）使得这一过程充满挑战。现有的方法难以将人类操作经验直接迁移到机器人上，因此需要一种与embodiment无关（embodiment-agnostic）的物体中心可操作affordance表示来解决这一根本性问题。

Method: 1) 利用最先进的3D Structure-from-Motion和手部网格重建技术，从第一人称人类视频中提取三类可操作affordance：视觉affordance（在哪里交互）、抓取affordance（如何抓取）和轨迹affordance（如何移动）。2) 构建EgoAffordance大规模数据集，包含204K视频片段、5.6M视觉affordance和11.6M抓取与轨迹affordance。3) 提出VLAff，一个基于大型视觉语言模型（VLM）的统一基础模型，学习所有可操作affordance之间的跨模态相关性，给定视觉观察和指令后同时生成视觉affordance热图、抓取姿态和运动轨迹，再利用3D场景信息将这些输出转化为可直接执行的动作。

Result: 通过大量实验证明，VLAff在视觉affordance预测任务上达到最先进性能，并能有效应用于真实机器人场景，包括零样本操作和affordance引导的机器人学习。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过学习embodiment-agnostic的物体中心可操作affordance（视觉、抓取、轨迹三模态），并利用大型视觉语言模型进行统一建模，可以有效弥合人类与机器人之间的embodiment差距，为实现可扩展的机器人操作学习提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Learning manipulation skills from human videos is promising for scalable robot learning. However, the embodiment mismatch between humans and robots makes this challenging. One promising solution is to learn object-centric actionable affordances that are embodiment-agnostic. In this work, we propose a framework that leverages egocentric human videos with state-of-the-art 3D Structure-from-Motion and hand mesh reconstruction to extract actionable affordances such as visual, grasp, and trajectory affordances that explicitly encode where to interact, how to grasp, and how to move. We construct EgoAffordance, a large-scale dataset comprising 204K episodes with 5.6M visual affordances and 11.6M grasp and trajectory affordances. Building on this, we introduce VLAff, a large vision-language model-based unified foundation model that learns cross-modal correlations across all actionable affordances. Given a visual observation and instruction, VLAff generates visual affordance heatmaps, grasp poses, and trajectories, which are then converted into directly executable actions by utilizing 3D scene information. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that VLAff not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on visual affordance prediction, but can also be effectively applied to real robot applications such as zero-shot manipulation and affordance-guided robot learning.

</details>


### [113] [A System for Train Condition Monitoring and Structural Health Assessment of Rail Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05221)
*Maximilian Posner,Martin Dazer,Daniela Lauer,Robert Winkler-Höhn,Mathilde Laporte,Tobias Herrmann,Martin Köppel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将结构传感器技术与AI数据分析相结合的新型实时车辆状态监测与碰撞检测方法，旨在解决干线铁路自动化运营（GoA4）中感知系统难以可靠识别碰撞和碾压事件的问题，覆盖碰撞检测、基于状态的维护和长期数据分析三大应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干线铁路中的全自动运行（GoA4）受限于严格的安全要求和开放运营环境的复杂性，部署进度落后于地铁系统。现有基于相机、雷达和激光雷达的感知系统虽能检测物体，但在可靠识别碰撞、冲击和碾压事件方面能力有限，迫切需要新的补充方案。

Method: 该框架将结构传感器技术与基于AI的数据分析相集成，实现实时的车辆条件监测和冲击检测。具体包含三大核心应用：(1)自动化检测冲击、结构损伤和碾压事件；(2)通过连续监测实现基于状态的维护；(3)利用长期数据分析支持车辆设计优化。

Result: 实验结果证明了所提出方法的可行性，展示了其在增强运营安全性、实现预测性维护策略以及支持干线铁路系统向全自动运营过渡方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 该结构传感器与AI融合的方法为干线铁路感知系统提供了碰撞与碾压事件检测的补充与增强能力，为推进干线铁路全自动运营（GoA4）提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The ongoing digitalization of rail systems and the increasing use of artificial intelligence (AI) are fundamentally transforming the design, operation, and maintenance of rail vehicles. While fully automated operation at Grade of Automation 4 (GoA4) is well established in metro systems, its deployment in mainline rail remains limited. This is primarily due to stringent safety requirements and the complexity of open operational environments. Current perception systems based on cameras, radar, and lidar are effective in detecting objects but provide limited capability for reliably identifying impacts, collisions, and driving-over events. This paper presents a novel approach for real-time vehicle condition monitoring and impact detection that integrates structural sensor technologies with AI-based data analysis. The proposed framework addresses three key applications: (1) automated detection of impacts, structural damage, and driving-over events, (2) condition-based maintenance enabled by continuous monitoring, and (3) long-term data analytics to support vehicle design optimization. The results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed approach and highlight its potential to enhance operational safety, enable predictive maintenance strategies, and support the transition toward fully automated operation in mainline rail systems

</details>


### [114] [Failing Gracefully: Mitigating Impact of Inevitable Robot Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05313)
*Duc M. Nguyen,Saad A. Ghani,Andrew Marshall,Allison Andreyev,Gregory J. Stein,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种评估服务机器人故障影响的新安全公式，量化故障对周围实体的影响概率与严重程度，并配套推出基于MuJoCo的FailBench仿真框架，用于在多种故障模式下系统研究机器人—环境交互，以平衡安全性与任务效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在家庭环境中与人类、宠物和日常物品共享空间，极易发生软件崩溃、硬件退化或不可预测交互等故障。虽然机器人学家努力减少故障，但部分故障不可避免，因此必须缓解故障后果以保障安全可靠的部署，而现有研究缺乏系统化评估故障影响的框架。

Method: 提出一种新颖的安全公式，同时评估故障期间机器人与周围实体之间高影响交互的概率及其后果严重程度；通过量化故障对不同实体的影响，使机器人能够在安全性与任务效率之间做出平衡的规划决策。同时构建FailBench——一个基于MuJoCo的仿真框架，用于在多种故障模式（包括传感问题和执行器故障）下研究机器人—环境交互。

Result: 安全公式与FailBench共同为在真实家庭环境中开发更安全、更鲁棒的运动规划和习得策略提供了基础。

Conclusion: 本文提出的安全公式与FailBench仿真框架共同奠定了开发面向真实家庭环境的更安全、更稳健运动规划与学习策略的基础，有助于实现服务机器人安全可靠的部署。

Abstract: Service robots operate in household environments shared with humans, pets, and everyday objects, where they are highly susceptible to failures such as software crashes, hardware degradation, or unpredictable interactions. While roboticists strive to minimize failures, some remain inevitable, making it critical to mitigate their potential consequences for safe and reliable deployment. This paper introduces a novel safety formulation that evaluates both the probability of impactful interactions between robots and surrounding entities during failures, and the severity of their outcomes. By quantifying the impact of failures on different entities, our approach enables robots to make informed planning decisions that balance safety with task efficiency. To support systematic evaluation, we also present FailBench, a MuJoCo-based simulation framework for studying robot-environment interactions under diverse failure modes, including sensing issues and actuator malfunctions. Together, our safety formulation and FailBench provide a foundation for developing safer and more robust motion plans and learned policies in real-world household environments.

</details>


### [115] [Unified Planning-Learning Framework for Robust UUV Navigation Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05365)
*Md Ether Deowan,Eleni Kelasidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向动态水下环境的UUV仅观测导航自主框架，融合持久占用地图构建、全局无碰撞规划与风险感知局部控制，通过RL策略、潜状态表征和行为树蒸馏等技术实现部分可观测条件下的鲁棒安全导航，并在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中通过多种子评估协议验证了优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下无人航行器（UUV）在动态环境中导航面临部分可观测性、传感器噪声和动态障碍物等挑战，现有方法难以在感知-规划-控制全链条上实现鲁棒且安全的自主导航，需要一种统一框架同时处理全局结构规划与局部反应式避碰。

Method: 提出observation-only自主框架，包含四部分：(1) 基于声纳与深度图像输入的持久占用地图构建；(2) 代价地图全局规划器（GP）提供长视野结构；(3) 强化学习（RL）策略处理短程跟踪与反应式避障，并学习紧凑潜状态表征编码环境结构、障碍物动力学与不确定性；(4) 行为树（BT）蒸馏结合分阶段监督提升安全性与训练稳定性，利用不确定性校准蒸馏机制（基于在线潜模型不确定性）对教师指导进行重加权，同时将碰撞时间（TTC）与无碰撞裕度（clearance）显式纳入规划与局部策略特征。评估在NVIDIA Isaac Sim高保真GPU加速仿真中进行多种子实验。

Result: 在NVIDIA Isaac Sim高保真动态仿真环境中，通过可复现的多种子评估协议，与BT-only及标准RL基线相比，该框架在动态条件下的鲁棒性和安全性均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条统一的混合规划-学习架构通用流程和可复现的评估方法论，能够在部分可观测条件下实现鲁棒的UUV自主导航，验证了不确定性校准蒸馏与显式安全线索融合对提升动态环境安全性的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents an observation-only autonomy framework for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles (UUVs) navigation in dynamic underwater environments that integrates persistent occupancy mapping, global clearance-aware planning, and risk-aware local control. The proposed pipeline constructs occupancy maps solely from onboard sonar and depth image observations, adapts a clearance-constrained global planner (GP) to provide long-horizon structure, and integrates a reinforcement learning (RL) policy to handle short-range tracking and reactive avoidance. To further support decision-making under partial observability, the system learns a compact latent state representation from onboard sensor data, encoding environmental structure, obstacle dynamics, and uncertainty. Behavior tree (BT) distillation with staged supervision is introduced to improve safety and training stability, while an uncertainty-calibrated distillation mechanism reweights teacher guidance using online latent-model uncertainty, emphasizing uncertain regimes during learning, with time-to-collision (TTC) and clearance cues remaining explicit in planning and local policy features. To demonstrate the efficacy of the framework, a reproducible multi-seed evaluation protocol is established in high-fidelity GPU-accelerated simulation using NVIDIA Isaac Sim, and performance is benchmarked against BT-only and standard RL baselines. The results obtained demonstrate improved robustness and safety under dynamic conditions, thus providing a general pipeline with a unified hybrid planning learning architecture and a reproducible methodology for robust UUV autonomy under partial observability.

</details>


### [116] [World-to-Wrist: Task-Conditioned Future Wrist Modeling for Fine-Grained Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05369)
*Yuhao Pan,Haosong Peng,Zhengshen Zhang,Zhengyang Yan,Yalun Dai,Fushuo Huo,Chujie Wang,Tianyu Qi,Xiucheng Wang,Nan Cheng,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出World-to-Wrist VLA (W2-VLA)，一种用于精细机器人操作任务的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过task-conditioned future wrist modeling（任务条件下的未来腕部建模）来利用腕部视角的独特作用。模型引入潜在建模token作为视觉语言模型和腕部预测器之间的紧凑接口，并配套提出W2-CoT合成管线生成结构化标注作为辅助监督。实验在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界任务上验证了在单臂和双臂场景下精细及接触敏感操作性能的提升，同时保持80 Hz以上的动作生成速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将主视角和腕部视角视为平行的视觉输入，忽略了它们在机器人操作中的不同角色。精细操作需要能够预测在全局任务上下文下腕部局部交互如何演变，因此需要一种能够对腕部未来状态进行任务条件建模的方法。

Method: W2-VLA包含三个核心组件：(1) 一套潜在建模token作为视觉语言模型与腕部预测器之间的紧凑接口；(2) 基于该接口和观测到的腕部历史，腕部预测器预测未来腕部潜在表征，并将其转化为未来感知的上下文用于动作预测；(3) W2-CoT合成管线生成结构化标注（描述操作进度、物理转换线索和腕部局部证据），为任务条件潜在接口提供辅助监督。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，W2-VLA在单臂和双臂设置下均改善了精细且接触敏感的操作性能，同时保持80 Hz以上的动作生成速率。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模腕部视角的未来状态并借助结构化标注进行辅助监督，W2-VLA有效地利用了对偶视角（主视角与腕部视角）的不同角色，提升了VLA模型在精细操作任务中的表现，且计算开销保持在实时要求的范围内。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often treat main-view and wrist-view observations as parallel visual inputs, overlooking their distinct roles in robot manipulation. Fine-grained manipulation, however, benefits from anticipating how wrist-local interactions may evolve under the global task context. To address this limitation, we present World-to-Wrist VLA (W2-VLA), a VLA model for fine-grained robot manipulation with task-conditioned future wrist modeling. Given current multi-view observations and a task instruction, W2-VLA contextualizes a set of latent modeling tokens as a compact interface between the vision-language model and the wrist predictor. Conditioned on this interface and the observed wrist history, the predictor forecasts future wrist latents, which are transformed into future-aware context for action prediction. In addition, we introduce W2-CoT, a synthesis pipeline that produces structured annotations describing manipulation progress, physical transition cues, and wrist-local evidence. These annotations provide auxiliary supervision that shapes the task-conditioned latent interface. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved fine-grained and contact-sensitive manipulation across both single-arm and bimanual settings, while maintaining action-generation rates above 80 Hz.

</details>


### [117] [Sliding Sensors: Configurable Confidence in State Estimation for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05410)
*Ella Walsh,Spencer Teetaert,Eric Diller,Timothy D. Barfoot,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过机械可重构传感（传感器在连续体机器人内纵向滑动）来主动塑造状态估计不确定性的方法，相比固定端部传感器，动态滑动传感器可降低全身形状估计误差，并在任务相关位置实现足够置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人在不确定环境中工作，需要准确的状态估计。然而估计不确定性在空间上天然非均匀，且全局精度并非总是首要目标，更重要的是在任务相关位置获得足够的置信度。现有固定传感器无法满足这一需求，因此需要一种能够重塑不确定性的感知方案。

Method: 提出机械可重构传感的概念：通过连续体机器人内部纵向平移传感器来改变测量位置，从而重塑状态估计的不确定度分布。文中展示了概念硬件设计以验证该方案的可行性，并通过对比固定端部传感器与随时间前后滑动的传感器方案来评估形状估计误差。

Result: 硬件设计验证了传感沿机器人纵向平移的可行性；实验表明改变传感器位置可以重新配置状态估计的置信度分布，且随时间前后滑动传感器相比单个固定端部传感器能显著降低全身形状估计误差。

Conclusion: 机械可重构传感为连续体机器人提供了一种主动塑造估计不确定性的新范式，能够在任务相关位置灵活调配感知资源，平衡全局精度与局部置信度需求。

Abstract: Continuum robots often operate in uncertain environments, where accurate state estimation is essential for safe interactions. Estimate uncertainty is inherently spatially non-uniform: confidence varies depending on where measurements are available. Global estimation accuracy is not always the top priority, but rather achieving sufficient confidence at task-relevant locations along the robot. This extended abstract introduces mechanically reconfigurable sensing enabling uncertainty-shaping in state estimation for continuum robots. We present a concept hardware design demonstrating the feasibility of longitudinal translation of a sensor within a continuum robot. We demonstrate that state estimation confidence can be reconfigured by varying the sensor location, and show a reduction of full-body shape estimation errors when sliding the sensor back and forth over time, compared to a single fixed tip sensor.

</details>


### [118] [ARGUS: Aligning Robot Scene Geometry Under Shifting Views with Large 3D Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05579)
*Rishik Sathua,Haonan Chen,Katherine Driggs-Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ARGUS，一个利用大规模3D视觉模型将任意相机视角的图像观测对齐到统一规范视角的预处理管线，以解决视觉运动策略中场景几何与视角纠缠的问题，从而提升策略在视角多样化数据集上的学习效率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉运动策略在机器人操作任务中表现出色，但常将场景几何与相机视角纠缠在一起，使得策略倾向于学习物体在图像中的像素位置而非任务空间中的真实位置。这种纠缠限制了策略从视角多样化的数据集（如DROID、BridgeV2）中学习和泛化到训练数据之外视角的能力。

Method: ARGUS是一种观察预处理管线，通过大规模3D视觉模型将来自任意相机视角的图像观测对齐到一个规范（canonical）视角，然后再将处理后的观测传递给下游的视觉运动策略，从而解耦视角信息与场景几何信息。

Result: 在从固定多视角相机配置到高度多样化相机布局的多种训练数据集上进行实验，ARGUS在有限视角和视角多样的训练体制下均持续优于先前方法。在效率比较中，ARGUS通过简化观测空间，比先前方法快4-6倍收敛到高成功率，表现出从视角多样数据中学习的能力。

Conclusion: 利用大规模3D视觉模型可以减少视觉运动策略的学习负担，使其能够更高效地从大规模、视角多样化的机器人数据集中进行学习，为构建可跨视角泛化的机器人操纵策略提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Large-scale visuomotor policies have demonstrated impressive performance across a wide range of robot manipulation tasks. However, despite this success, manipulation polices often entangle scene geometry with the corresponding viewpoint, learning where objects lie in an image rather than where it lies in the task space. This entanglement inherently limits the corresponding policy's ability to learn from viewpoint-diverse datasets (ex. DROID, BridgeV2) and generalize beyond the viewpoints captured in their training data. In this work, we present ARGUS, an observation pre-processing pipeline that uses large-scale 3D vision models to align image observations from arbitrary camera viewpoints into a canonical viewpoint before passing it to downstream visuomotor policies. Experiments across training datasets with varying levels of viewpoint diversity, from fixed multi-view camera configurations to highly varied camera placements, show that our method consistently outperforms prior approaches across both limited-view and view-diverse training regimes. In efficiency comparisons, ARGUS demonstrates an ability to learn from view-diverse data, converging to high success rates 4-6x faster than previous methods by leveraging a simplified observation space. Overall, our findings show that leveraging large-scale 3D vision models reduces the learning burden on visuomotor policies, enabling more efficient learning from large-scale, viewpoint-diverse robot datasets.

</details>


### [119] [PathCover: A Fast Convex Decomposition along a Path via Randomized Iterative Space Partitioning (RISP) on Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05586)
*Kunal S. Narkhede,Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Guoquan Huang,Ioannis Poulakakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PathCover是一个由RISP随机算法驱动的自主机器人导航框架，能从原始点云数据直接以期望线性时间构建凸多面体走廊，较现有方法实现数量级加速，并已在四足机器人上完成实物验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有走廊生成器无法满足自主机器人导航中实时性、传感器速率级别的计算约束，成为轨迹规划的瓶颈。

Method: 提出RISP随机算法，在温和的概率消去条件下以期望线性时间从原始点云构建凸多面体；PathCover框架生成重叠的无障碍多面体序列来约束MPC和轨迹优化，并保证沿参考路径的有限步终止与连续进展。

Result: 在合成与真实LiDAR数据集上较最先进方法实现数量级加速，同时保持相当的走廊体积；通过高保真四旋翼仿真和搭载实时LiDAR感知的四足机器人实物部署完成验证。

Conclusion: PathCover在速度与安全性的兼顾上取得突破，可在实时传感器速率下生成高质量无障碍走廊，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous robot navigation requires the rapid generation of obstacle-free regions for trajectory planning. However, existing corridor generators struggle to meet real-time, sensor-rate computational constraints. To resolve this bottleneck, we introduce PathCover, a framework driven by RISP; a novel randomized algorithm that constructs convex polytopes directly from raw point cloud data in expected linear time under a mild probabilistic elimination condition. PathCover generates sequences of overlapping, obstacle-free polytopes that safely constrain downstream MPC and trajectory optimization. We mathematically guarantee that the algorithm terminates in finite steps while ensuring continuous progress along any obstacle-free reference path. Extensive benchmarks on synthetic and real-world LiDAR datasets demonstrate an order-of-magnitude speedup over state-of-the-art methods while maintaining comparable corridor volumes. The complete pipeline is validated via high-fidelity quadrotor simulations and physical deployment on a quadrupedal robot navigating constrained environments using live LiDAR perception.

</details>


### [120] [Search-Aided Joint Agent-Environment Reinforcement Learning for Robust Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding with Rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05588)
*He Jiang,Jingtian Yan,Yulun Zhang,Yimin Tang,Tanishq Duhan,Rishi Veerapaneni,Guillaume Sartoretti,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LMAPF-R2这一更贴近真实仓库场景的终身多智能体路径规划模型，引入鲁棒安全约束和原地旋转约束，并提出了搜索辅助联合强化学习（SJRL）方法，通过Causal PIBT搜索增强神经策略并联合优化智能体与环境策略，在多种高密度地图及混合现实仓库环境中显著优于强基线搜索规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的终身多智能体路径规划（LMAPF）学习方法大多采用过度简化的运动学假设，忽略了真实世界应用中至关重要的运动约束（如安全约束和原地旋转约束），导致在真实仓库系统等场景中性能不佳。

Method: 提出Search-Aided Joint Reinforcement Learning (SJRL)方法：(1) 将神经策略与Causal PIBT（单步搜索规划器）结合，用于解决智能体碰撞并传播意图；(2) 引入统一的RL框架，联合优化智能体策略与环境策略，其中环境策略通过反向Dijkstra搜索学习图边代价，提供全局移动引导。

Result: 在多个高密度地图上，SJRL相比强搜索基线Causal-PIBT取得了显著改进；并在包含8台物理机器人和248台虚拟机器人的挑战性混合现实仓库环境中得到验证。

Conclusion: SJRL通过搜索增强的联合强化学习范式，有效解决了引入真实安全与旋转约束后带来的协调难度提升问题，为现实仓库场景下的LMAPF提供了可行且性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding (LMAPF) requires repeatedly planning collision-free paths for agents that continuously receive new goals upon reaching their current ones. While many learning-based planners have been proposed for LMAPF, most rely on oversimplified kinematic assumptions that may overlook motion constraints critical to real-world performance. In this work, we study a more realistic LMAPF model derived from many real-world automated warehouse systems, termed LMAPF-R2, which incorporates robust safety constraints and in-place rotation constraints. These constraints substantially increase coordination difficulty, particularly in highly constrained spaces. To address these challenges, we propose Search-Aided Joint Reinforcement Learning (SJRL). We first augment neural policies with Causal PIBT, a single-step search-based planner that resolves agents' collisions and propagates their intentions. We then introduce a unified RL formulation that jointly optimizes agent and environment policies, where the environment policy learns graph edge costs to provide global movement guidance via backward Dijkstra search. Experiments demonstrate that SJRL achieves significant improvements over the strong search-based planner, Causal-PIBT, across multiple high-density maps. We further validate SJRL in a challenging mixed-reality warehouse environment with 8 physical robots and 248 virtual robots.

</details>


### [121] [KILVO: Kinematic-Inertial-LiDAR-Visual Odometry with Robust Multimodal Adaptation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05647)
*Jixin Gao,Fucheng Liu,Teng Zhang,Fusheng Zha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KILVO是一种面向人形机器人的运动学-惯性-LiDAR-视觉里程计方法，通过异步-顺序混合误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波器（ESIKF）融合关节编码器、IMU、LiDAR和相机等多模态传感器，实现了高精度、高效且对传感器故障具有鲁棒性的状态估计，并在公共数据集和多种真实人形机器人上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的复杂运动模式和多样化真实场景对状态估计提出了更高要求，现有融合方法未能充分利用人形机器人上常备的多种传感器（关节编码器、IMU、LiDAR、相机），且在多模态融合、传感器故障鲁棒性和输出频率方面存在不足。

Method: 提出KILVO框架，在异步-顺序混合误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波器（ESIKF）中融合四类传感器：惯性数据用于预测，腿部运动学以高频率异步处理提供本体感知约束，外感知通过顺序更新——先注册LiDAR点获得几何先验，再通过光度误差更新视觉分量。框架设计了多模态自适应机制以应对传感器故障，并构建了紧凑的接触估计模块，无需额外传感器即可与状态估计共享信息。

Result: 在公共数据集和多种真实人形机器人、多种步态模式和场景上进行了广泛实验，KILVO在精度、效率和输出频率方面均达到高度竞争力，且在传感器退化和故障下表现出强鲁棒性，优于现有的最先进融合方法。

Conclusion: KILVO相比现有最先进融合方法更适合人形机器人，其多模态自适应融合和紧凑接触估计模块为复杂真实场景下的人形机器人状态估计提供了可靠方案，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: This article presents a kinematic-inertial-LiDAR-visual odometry for humanoid robots, called KILVO. Tailored to the platform features, requirements, and real-world complexity, it fully utilizes the sensors commonly equipped on humanoid robots, including joint encoders, IMU, LiDAR, and camera, within an asynchronous-sequential hybrid error-state iterated Kalman filter (ESIKF). Specifically, inertial data are used for prediction, leg kinematics are processed asynchronously at a high rate and provide proprioceptive constraints, while exteroception is updated sequentially, first by registering LiDAR points for geometric priors and then by updating the visual component via photometric errors. Moreover, the framework is elaborately designed with multimodal adaptation for resilience to sensor failures. A compact contact estimation module is also developed, sharing information with state estimation without additional sensors. Extensive experiments on public datasets and in the real world across multiple humanoid robots, gait patterns, and scenarios demonstrate that KILVO achieves highly competitive accuracy, efficiency, and output rates, with strong robustness against sensor degradation and failures, making it more suitable for humanoid robots than state-of-the-art fusion methods. Our code and datasets are released on GitHub.

</details>


### [122] [JoyAI-RA 0.5: Scaling Robot Manipulation Learning via Dual Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05674)
*JoyAI-RA Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JoyAI-RA 0.5是一个通用的视觉-语言-世界-动作(VLWA)框架，通过双重动作对齐（隐式对齐与显式对齐）让机器人策略能够从异构数据源（人类第一视角视频、仿真和真实机器人）中学习操作技能，在真实AgiBot基准上表现优异，且人类视频数据量越大任务分数持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人数据稀缺，通用策略需要从异构数据源学习，但这些数据在监督信号和具身形式上差异巨大——动作标签缺失或互不兼容。人类第一视角数据规模最大但与机器人数据差异最大，朴素数据混合会导致负迁移而非知识共享，亟需一种能跨数据源对齐和利用异构数据的框架。

Method: 提出JoyAI-RA 0.5 VLWA框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 隐式动作对齐——从视觉转换中推断潜在动作，使无动作标签的人类、仿真和机器人数据引导潜在动作条件的世界模型学习物理动态；(2) 显式动作对齐——通过规范动作表示和相机帧块相对末端执行器动作，将可靠的人类和机器人轨迹统一到一致的物理动作空间；(3) 内外循环强化阶段——结合高效任务适应与基础策略持续改进。

Result: 在真实AgiBot基准上，JoyAI-RA在已见任务和未见变化任务上均表现优异。任务分数随人类第一视角预训练数据量的增加而持续提升，且在最大数据规模下仍未出现平台期。

Conclusion: 丰富但弱标注的人类经验可以被转化为可迁移的训练信号，使人类视频不仅是辅助数据源，而是可以作为提升操作能力的主要扩展轴线，为利用大规模人类数据扩展机器人操作能力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Robot data is scarce, so generalist policies need to learn from heterogeneous sources, including human egocentric video, simulation, and real robots, which differ in supervision and embodiment, with action labels missing or mutually incompatible. Human egocentric data scale best but sit farthest from robot data, and naive pooling causes negative transfer rather than knowledge sharing. We propose JoyAI-RA 0.5, a generalist Vision-Language-World-Action (VLWA) framework that couples physical world-dynamics priors with visual semantics and scales manipulation learning across such data via dual action alignment. Implicit action alignment infers latent actions from visual transitions, enabling action-free human, simulation, and robot data to guide a latent-action-conditioned world model in learning physical dynamics. Explicit alignment grounds reliable human and robot trajectories in a unified physical action space through a canonical action representation and camera-frame chunk-relative end-effector actions. An inner-outer-loop reinforcement stage then pairs efficient task adaptation with foundation-policy improvement. On a real-world AgiBot benchmark, JoyAI-RA performs strongly on both seen tasks and unseen variations. The task score improves consistently as the volume of human egocentric pretraining data increases and shows no sign of plateauing at our largest scale. This suggests that abundant but weakly labeled human experience can be converted into a transferable training signal, making human video not merely a weak auxiliary source but a primary axis along which manipulation capability can be scaled. Project page can be found at https://joyai-ra-05.github.io/.

</details>


### [123] [Nonvisual Classification of Ground-Condition by Artificial Proprioception in an Amoeba-Inspired Autonomous Walking Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05684)
*Hyoto Yamaguchi,Zenji Yatabe,Seiya Kasai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于多模态传感与非视觉方法的自主行走机器人地面条件分类系统，利用三轴加速度计、八个足部压力传感器和储备池计算（RC）实现人工本体感觉，从而无需图像传感即可高精度区分平坦与粗糙地面，并实现行走步态的现场切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地面分类方法依赖视觉传感与图像处理，计算开销大且在动态行走机器人中易受运动干扰。为了在类似变形虫的自主行走机器人上实现鲁棒、轻量且非视觉的地面条件感知，作者提出利用人工本体感觉（加速度与足压多模态融合）并结合储备池计算进行分类。

Method: 系统集成了三类传感模态：三轴加速度计捕捉机体动态、八个足部压力传感器感知地面反作用力，并使用储备池计算（reservoir computing, RC）作为时序信号处理与分类器。RC对传感器输出的时序波动具有鲁棒性，能在四足机器人动态行走产生的强干扰下完成地面条件分类。该方法还进一步分析了各传感器模态对分类准确率的贡献度。

Result: 实验结果表明，即使在四足机器人行走产生的较大传感器输出波动下，系统仍能以高精度区分平坦与粗糙地面。同时，通过在机器人上实现根据地面条件即时切换行走步态，验证了该系统的实际可行性。此外，对单个传感器模态的消融分析阐明了不同传感源在分类中的相对贡献。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，基于多模态传感（加速度+足压）与储备池计算的人工本体感觉方法，能够在不依赖视觉的情况下有效实现行走机器人对地面条件的鲁棒分类，并支持基于环境的步态自适应切换，为低功耗、无视觉的自主机器人感知提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Nonvisual classification of ground condition based on a multimodal sensing approach was investigated for an amoeba-inspired autonomous walking robot. To classify ground condition without image sensing and processing, we implemented artificial proprioception by integrating a three-axis accelerometer, eight foot pressure sensors, and reservoir computing (RC). Even when large fluctuations in the sensor outputs are caused by dynamic motions of a four-legged robot in walking, our system can classify the ground condition, flat or rough, with high accuracy. We demonstrate on-site switching of walking gait depending on ground condition in the robot. We also discuss the contribution of each sensor to ground condition classification.

</details>


### [124] [Hijacking Robots with a Piece of Paper: A Systematic Study of Physical Prompt Injection in VLM-Controlled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05715)
*S. M . Bhagya P. Samarakoon,M. A. Viraj J. Muthugala,W. K. R. Sachinthana,Mohan Rajesh Elara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了针对视觉语言模型（VLM）控制的分拣机器人的物理提示注入攻击，提出了四类攻击分类法，在三个前沿VLM上进行了5670次实验，发现攻击成功率在5.0%-29.4%之间，并评估了三种简单防御措施的有效性（75%-100%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM越来越多地被部署为机器人系统中的规划器，将自然语言命令转换为基于视觉场景理解的可执行动作。这种感知与指令执行的紧密耦合引入了新的攻击面：放置在机器人视野内的对抗性文本可以作为间接提示注入到VLM的推理栈中，这是一个尚未被系统研究的安全威胁。

Method: 提出四类攻击分类法（间接标志、任务重定义、权威冒充、冲突注入），构建20个攻击提示的基准测试集，覆盖三种物理场景布局和三种命令表述（在目标特异性和规则明确性上有所变化）。在GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Qwen3-VL-32B三个前沿VLM上进行了5670次实验，并对推理轨迹进行分析，同时评估三种简单缓解措施（基于提示的防御、两阶段验证、预处理文本掩码）。

Result: 攻击成功率分别为GPT-4o 27.0%、Gemini 2.5 Flash 29.4%、Qwen3-VL-32B 5.0%。权威冒充和否定类攻击可跨模型迁移。成功入侵几乎总是有意识的行为（99.9%承认率），且不同模型采用不同的防御机制（Gemini显式拒绝，GPT-4o感知性忽视）。三种防御措施分别达到75-100%、85-100%和100%的有效性。

Conclusion: VLM控制的操作系统确实容易受到人类可读物理标识的攻击，但简单防御措施可以大幅降低风险。防御选择涉及权衡：现有防御在基准测试中保留了通用任务能力，但可能削弱需要阅读场景内标签的任务。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as planners in robotic systems, where they translate natural-language commands into executable actions grounded in visual scene understanding. This tight coupling between perception and instruction-following introduces a new attack surface: adversarial text placed within the robot's visual field can act as an indirect prompt injection into the VLM's reasoning stack. We present a systematic study of physical prompt injection attacks against VLM-controlled sorting, introducing a four-category taxonomy, indirect signage, task redefinition, authority impersonation, and conflict injection, instantiated as a benchmark of 20 attack prompts evaluated across three physical scene layouts and three command formulations that vary in destination specificity and rule explicitness. Across 5,670 trials on three frontier VLMs (GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen3-VL-32B), attacks succeed at 27.0%, 29.4%, and 5.0% respectively, with authority-impersonating and negation attacks transferring across all three models. Analysis of reasoning traces reveals that successful compromise is nearly always conscious (99.9% acknowledgment rate), and that models defend through structurally different mechanisms, explicit rejection for Gemini, perceptual inattention for GPT-4o. We evaluate three simple mitigations: prompt-based defense (75-100% effective, model-dependent), two-stage verification (85-100%), and pre-processing text masking (100%). Our findings show that VLM-controlled manipulation is meaningfully vulnerable to human-readable physical signage, and that simple defenses substantially reduce risk, though defense choice involves trade-offs. The defenses preserve general task capabilities in our benchmark, but they may impair tasks that require reading in-scene labels.

</details>


### [125] [ATP: Anatomical Torque with Passivity-based Control Framework for Safe Upper-Limb Exoskeleton Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05723)
*Yu Chen,Gong Chen,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ATP（Anatomical Torque with Passivity-Based Control）方法，用于安全的上肢外骨骼辅助。该方法通过可扩展的肌肉骨骼仿真框架训练统一强化学习肌肉控制器，生成解剖参考力矩；结合在线力矩优化与异常评分实现安全舒适辅助；并通过交互力矩控制器配合能量罐保证无源性。仿真和真实实验验证了准确跟踪、泛化能力及肌电研究中最高48%的目标肌肉活动降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解剖知识辅助主要在周期性、负重运动的下肢外骨骼中得到研究，其对力矩精度要求较低；而上肢运动复杂且非周期，对力矩精度要求高，将解剖辅助扩展到上肢仍具挑战，因此需要一种安全且能泛化的上肢外骨骼辅助方法。

Method: (1) 提出可扩展的肌肉骨骼仿真框架，训练统一强化学习肌肉控制器，能够跨上肢运动泛化并生成解剖参考力矩，无需复杂生物力学计算；(2) 在线力矩优化机制适应多样运动、抑制肌腱引起的力矩尖峰，并集成学习式异常评分以确保安全舒适辅助；(3) 通过缆驱柔顺外骨骼的交互力矩控制器提供辅助，不限制预定轨迹，并利用能量罐保证系统无源性，提供力矩跟踪和系统无源性的理论保证。

Result: 仿真和真实实验表明该方法在长时间运动序列中准确跟踪力矩并泛化到实时人体运动；控制器在保持无源性的同时实现精确力矩跟踪，且在能量罐补充后能恢复跟踪。五项参与者EMG研究表明，与重力补偿和开环辅助相比，在静态和动态任务中目标肌肉活动均降低，在动态多关节任务中相对于无外骨骼运动最高降低48%。

Conclusion: ATP方法成功将解剖辅助从下肢扩展到复杂非周期的上肢运动，通过强化学习生成解剖参考力矩、在线力矩优化和保无源性控制，实现了安全、舒适且泛化能力强的上肢外骨骼辅助，并在肌电实验中验证了显著的肌肉活动降低效果。

Abstract: Providing assistance across diverse movements is a central objective of exoskeletons, and anatomical knowledge can enable responsive support that generalizes across tasks. However, anatomical assistance has mainly been studied for lower-limb exoskeletons, where periodic, weight-bearing motions impose lower demands on torque precision. Extending such assistance to complex, nonperiodic upper-limb movements remains challenging. This paper proposes Anatomical Torque with Passivity-Based Control (ATP) for safe upper-limb exoskeleton assistance. First, a scalable musculoskeletal simulation framework trains a unified reinforcement-learning muscle controller that generalizes across upper-limb movements and generates anatomical reference torques without complex biomechanical computations. Second, an online torque-refinement scheme adapts the reference to diverse movements, suppresses tendon-induced spikes, and incorporates a learned anomaly score for safe and comfortable assistance. Third, an interaction torque controller delivers assistance through a cable-driven compliant exoskeleton without constraining motion to predefined trajectories, while an energy tank preserves passivity with theoretical guarantees on torque tracking and system passivity. Simulations and real-world experiments show accurate tracking of long-duration motion sequences and generalization to real-time human movements. The controller achieves accurate torque tracking while preserving passivity and resumes tracking after energy-tank replenishment. An EMG study with five participants further shows reduced target-muscle activity during static and dynamic tasks compared with gravity compensation and open-loop assistance, with reductions of up to 48% relative to movement without the exoskeleton in a dynamic multi-joint task.

</details>


### [126] [Near-sensor Computing for Rapid Visuotactile Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05725)
*Zhengying Zhu,Ruilin Zhang,Runze Hu,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于近传感器计算框架的触觉传感器系统，将光谱泊松求解器实现为全流式硬件流水线，以确定性延迟、低功耗（347 mW）和高精度（深度误差仅为峰值接触深度的0.17%）的方式重建稠密接触几何，并将机器人保护性反射回路延迟从169.9 ms降至28.3 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉触觉传感器通过宿主处理器进行接触几何重建，导致功耗高、数据传输延迟和调度延迟不确定，限制了机器人系统的感知与响应速度。

Method: 实现近传感器计算框架，将光谱泊松求解器设计为全流式硬件流水线。该流水线无数据相关分支和迭代收敛过程，具有确定性延迟，工作频率166 MHz。

Result: 流水线在128x128帧上，首个深度值在收到首个输入像素后35,107个周期（固定延迟0.211 ms）产出；在15种接触几何中，重建深度与双精度参考的偏差为峰值接触深度的0.17%；基于片上决策的机器人保护性反射回路耗时28.3±4.9 ms，远优于基于主机的169.9±27.8 ms；核心逻辑功耗估计为347 mW。

Conclusion: 近传感器重建能够在适合快速机器人接触响应的时间尺度上提供准确、节能且确定性的触觉几何信息，为高性能机器人触觉感知提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Visuotactile sensors reconstruct dense contact geometry from measured surface gradients, but host-based processing increases power consumption and introduces data-transfer delays and variable scheduling latency, limiting the sensing and response speed of robotic systems. To address these limitations, we implement a near-sensor computing framework that includes a spectral Poisson solver as a fully streaming hardware pipeline. The computational core logic has an estimated power consumption of 347 mW and achieves high throughput without data-dependent branching or iterative convergence, thereby providing deterministic latency. Operating at 166 MHz, the pipeline produces the first depth value of each 128x128 frame 35,107 cycles after receiving the first input pixel, corresponding to a fixed latency of 0.211 ms. Across 15 contact geometries, the reconstructed depths differ from a double-precision reference by 0.17 % of the peak contact depth. On-chip decisions based on these reconstructions close a robot protective reflex loop in 28.3 +/- 4.9 ms, compared with 169.9 +/- 27.8 ms for an equivalent host-based loop using the same actuator. These results demonstrate that near-sensor reconstruction can provide accurate, energy-efficient, and deterministic tactile geometry on timescales suitable for rapid robotic contact responses.

</details>


### [127] [In-Context VLA: Endowing Vision-Language-Action Models with Language via In-Context Post-Training and Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05738)
*Jiarui Yang,Wen Huang,Jiale Zhang,Maowei Hu,Hang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的VLA框架，反对在策略中生成自由形式的文本链式推理（CoT），认为这会破坏低层控制性能；转而通过上下文后训练和代理工具接口，让VLA学会"消费"而非"生成"接地语言，从而在多个仿真和真实机器人任务上取得SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型普遍采用行为克隆训练，仅依赖静态图像和固定指令；虽然注入文本CoT看似能提升推理能力，但作者发现自由形式的CoT会引入未接地推理、破坏闭环时序，并且推理token与动作token训练目标冲突，导致策略"叙述而非行动"。因此需要一种让VLA具备语言理解能力而非语言生成能力的新方法。

Method: 提出OURMETHOD框架，包含两个核心组件：(i) 上下文后训练（in-context post-training），将感知证据以结构化上下文注入，仅对动作进行监督；(ii) 代理工具使用接口（agentic tool-use interface），策略可主动查询开放词汇检测器、单目深度估计和视觉语言模型以获取任务相关信息。数据引擎生成多样化、释义化、证据条件的空间描述，使策略学会解析从未逐字见过的语言。

Result: 在RoboCasa-GR1、SimplerEnv、LIBERO仿真基准以及8个真实机器人操控任务上，与匹配配置下的CoT方法相比，该方法在性能和效率两方面均一致达到SOTA结果。

Conclusion: VLA所需的不是生成语言的能力，而是消费接地语言的能力；通过结构化上下文注入与主动信息获取接口，可以显著提升VLA在通用操控任务中的表现，且比依赖CoT的方法更高效。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become the dominant recipe for generalist manipulation, yet they are almost universally trained by behavior cloning: a policy imitates expert action chunks conditioned on a static image and a fixed instruction. A natural remedy is to inject explicit reasoning through textual chain-of-thought (CoT). We show, both empirically and analytically, that free-form textual CoT degrades low-level control: the reasoning it produces is ungrounded, its latency breaks closed-loop timing, and, crucially, the reasoning and action tokens are optimized against conflicting objectives so that the policy learns to narrate rather than to act. We argue that what a VLA needs is not the ability to generate language, but the ability to consume grounded language. To this end we introduce \textbf{\ourmethod{}}, a framework that endows a VLA with language competence through (i) in-context post-training, in which perceptual evidence is injected as structured context and the model is supervised only on actions, and (ii) an agentic tool-use interface, in which the policy queries open-vocabulary detectors, monocular depth, and a vision--language model to actively acquire task-relevant information. Rather than emitting a single templated caption, our data engine produces diverse, paraphrased, and evidence-conditioned spatial descriptions, so that the policy learns to interpret language it has never seen verbatim. Across the RoboCasa-GR1, SimplerEnv, and LIBERO simulation benchmarks, together with 8 real-world robot manipulation tasks, our method consistently achieves SOTA results in both performance and efficiency when compared with CoT-based approaches under matched configurations.

</details>


### [128] [Acoustic-driven millimetric helical robot: ultrasonic synergistic manipulation in confined fluidic environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05746)
*Hanlin Wang,Xin Wang,Xinwei Wei,Jiaxu Liu,Le Wang,Shengze Cai,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协同多声场方法，通过声辐射力与声流效应的协同作用，实现对毫米级螺旋机器人的可控运动与推进增强，并在体外猪静脉血管中验证了其导航和运动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声场驱动的操控策略在微纳米尺度对象上已得到广泛应用，但将其扩展到毫米级机器人时，在受限生物环境中推进效率不足，限制了其应用前景。

Method: 采用协同多声场方法，结合声辐射力与声流流动的协同作用驱动毫米级螺旋机器人；通过多物理场仿真捕获机器人在组合声场下的动力学行为，并进行实验验证。

Result: 实验验证了毫米级螺旋机器人在平面导航、倾斜爬升和垂直运动方面的运动能力；半自主导航实验表明超声协同显著提升了机动性；在猪静脉血管中的体外测试证实协同声场支持单向和往复运动。

Conclusion: 该研究为将声学微操控扩展到毫米尺度提供了机理层面的见解，并支持需要灵活可控机器人移动性的生物医学应用。

Abstract: Acoustic field-driven manipulation provides a non-contact and non-invasive strategy for controlling microscale and nanoscale objects, yet its extension to millimeter-scale robots was limited by insufficient propulsion efficiency in confined biological environments. Here, a coordinated multi-acoustic-field approach is introduced, which harnesses the synergistic action of acoustic radiation forces and acoustic streaming flows to enable controlled locomotion of millimeter-scale helical robots and enhance propulsion. Multiphysics simulations captured the dynamics of millimeter-scale helical robots under combined acoustic fields, and experimental validation demonstrated their locomotion capabilities, including planar navigation, inclined climbing, and vertical motion. Semi-autonomous navigation experiments further confirmed that ultrasonic synergy substantially improved maneuverability. In vitro tests in porcine venous vessels demonstrated that coordinated acoustic fields supported both unidirectional and reciprocating motion under biologically relevant confinement. These findings provide mechanistic insight into scaling acoustic micromanipulation to the millimetre regime and support biomedical applications requiring versatile and controllable robotic mobility.

</details>


### [129] [XEWorld: Can Action-Conditioned World Models Generalize to Unseen Robot Embodiments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05799)
*Yixiang Chen,Jiabing Yang,Yuan Xu,Qisen Ma,Keji He,Peiyan Li,Kai Wang,Ziheng He,Xiangnan Wu,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出XEWorld，一个用于世界模型的跨形态（cross-embodiment）测试平台，通过评估从未见过的机器人（held-out robots）来检验世界模型是否真正学习物理动力学还是仅记忆视觉模式。研究发现当前模型本质上是2D视觉模式匹配器，泛化由视觉相似性驱动而非物理运动学相似性，无法将抽象关节动作转化为连贯视觉轨迹，也难以从静态初始观测预测动态视觉变化。要零样本渲染未见形态必须依赖像素空间动作和显式时空对齐等强接地线索；即使通过少样本适应绕过零样本障碍，强制的外观恢复也会导致已见形态的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对动作条件世界模型的评估仅限于训练阶段见过的机器人，无法区分模型是真正捕获了物理动力学还是仅仅记忆了视觉模式。作者旨在回答"模型能否忠实渲染它从未见过的机器人"这一关键问题，从而揭示跨形态泛化的真实瓶颈。

Method: 提出了XEWorld，一个受控的跨形态世界模型测试平台，通过在物理相同的场景中评估held-out机器人来隔离形态（embodiment）变量。采用系统化分析方法，对比不同世界模型架构在零样本跨形态渲染和少样本适应场景下的表现，并分析视觉相似性与物理运动学相似性对泛化的影响。

Result: 发现当前模型普遍存在架构性瓶颈：它们是2D视觉模式匹配器，泛化取决于视觉相似性而非物理运动学相似性。模型难以将抽象数字关节动作翻译为连贯视觉轨迹，也无法从静态初始观测预测动态视觉变化。零样本渲染未见形态必须依赖强接地线索（像素空间动作、显式时空对齐）；少样本适应虽可绕过零样本障碍，但会引发已见形态的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 现有世界模型无法将学到的物理动力学应用到新的视觉外观上，真正的跨形态泛化需要架构创新，将视觉外观与底层物理动力学解耦。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models are promising learned simulators for robotic manipulation, yet evaluating them exclusively on training robots fails to reveal whether they capture physical dynamics or merely memorize visual patterns. To answer whether a model can faithfully render a robot it has never seen, we introduce XEWorld, a controlled cross-embodiment testbed for world models that isolates embodiments by evaluating held-out robots within physically identical scenes. Our systematic analysis uncovers a shared architectural bottleneck: current models act primarily as 2D visual pattern matchers whose generalization is governed by visual similarity rather than physical kinematic similarity. Driven by this limitation, they struggle to translate abstract numeric joint actions into coherent visual trajectories, and fail to predict dynamic visual changes from static initial observations. Consequently, successfully rendering an unseen embodiment zero-shot strictly requires heavily grounded cues, specifically pixel-space actions and explicit spatial-temporal alignment. Even when bypassing this zero-shot barrier via few-shot adaptation, the forced appearance recovery triggers catastrophic forgetting of seen embodiments. Together, these failures expose a critical inability to apply learned physical dynamics to novel visual appearances, highlighting that achieving true cross-embodiment generalization requires architectural innovations that decouple visual appearance from underlying physical dynamics.

</details>


### [130] [Coordinated Multi-Robot Disassembly for Makespan Optimization of Large-Scale Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05830)
*Niklas Hargus,Andreas Orthey,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoMuDi，一种面向多机器人拆卸任务的任务与运动规划方法，通过复合任务建模和时间约束传播协调机器人运动，并集成ST-RRT*规划器最小化完工时间。实验表明在多达49个零件和9个机器人的装配场景中，CoMuDi能高效求解并降低机器人空闲时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人拆卸任务要求机器人在受限工作空间中协同运动，现有方法难以在保证无碰撞的同时高效协调多机器人的任务分配与运动规划，导致整体完工时间较长、成功率较低。

Method: CoMuDi以机器人团队、装配体及依赖图作为输入，创建拾取、放置和退出动作的复合任务；通过传播时间约束确保每个机器人尽可能早地开始和结束任务并避免碰撞；集成空间-时间RRT*（ST-RRT*）规划器使单个任务最小化到达时间，从而最小化整体makespan。

Result: 对比CoMuDi使用ST-RRT*与RRT*在不同时间约束下的性能，结果表明CoMuDi结合ST-RRT*能获得更高成功率并最小化makespan；在多达49个零件和9个机器人的六个装配体上测试，CoMuDi生成的路径展现出低空闲时间，证明其能可靠求解大规模装配。

Conclusion: CoMuDi能在大规模多机器人拆卸任务中实现可靠规划，通过时间约束传播与ST-RRT*的集成有效提升成功率和时间效率，为受限空间中多机器人协调拆卸提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot task and motion planning for disassembly tasks requires robots to operate in confined workspaces while coordinating their motions with other robots. To tackle this problem, we propose a planning method called coordinated multi-robot disassembly (CoMuDi). CoMuDi coordinates a team of robots for disassembly tasks. The input is a team of robots, an assembly of objects, and a dependency graph. Based on this information, we create compound tasks for pick, place, and exit motions. By propagating temporal constraints, we ensure that each robot can start and end their tasks as early as possible while avoiding collisions with nearby robots. By integrating the space-time RRT* planner (ST-RRT*) into CoMuDi, we ensure that individual tasks minimize arrival time and thereby help us minimize overall makespan. We compare the performance of CoMuDi using both ST-RRT* and RRT* planners with varying time bounds, demonstrating that the combination of CoMuDi and ST-RRT* leads to a higher success rate while minimizing makespan. Finally, we evaluate CoMuDi on six assemblies with up to 49 pieces and up to 9 robots. In those scenarios, we show that CoMuDi returns robot paths that exhibit low idle times, thereby demonstrating that CoMuDi can reliably solve large-scale assemblies.

</details>


### [131] [SkillMemo: Expert-guided Skill Memory Framework for Compositional Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05970)
*Changyuan Wang,Chubin Zhang,Zhenyu Wu,Runhao Li,Angyuan Ma,Ke Chao,Yinan Liang,Xiuwei Xu,Ziwei Wang,Yansong Tang,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了SkillMemo框架，通过MoE架构隐式分解长程演示为潜在原子技能，并构建技能级情景记忆库，在推理时检索相关技能先验以增强DP和VLA模型的动作预测，在模拟和真实任务上超越π0.5，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉运动模型（如DP和VLA）在机器人操作任务上表现良好，但其潜力受限于大规模具身轨迹数据的稀缺，导致在分布外场景中组合泛化能力不足，难以捕获可复用的技能结构。

Method: SkillMemo框架包含两部分：(1) 基于专家混合（MoE）架构的专家引导轨迹分割模块，通过学习的门控系数隐式将长程轨迹划分为不同的技能原语；(2) 技能级情景记忆架构，将紧凑的技能表示存储为可检索的键值对。推理时，记忆库检索最相关的技能原语并与当前门控分布融合，为动作预测提供上下文先验。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实世界操作任务上的大量实验表明，SkillMemo一致性地增强了DP和VLA骨干模型的性能，取得了SOTA结果，超过π0.5，并对未见过的任务配置展现出强大的组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过隐式技能分解和动态情景记忆机制，SkillMemo有效缓解了具身轨迹数据稀缺的问题，赋予现有具身策略模型更强的技能复用能力和组合泛化能力，为具身智能体的长期学习提供了一种可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Embodied visuomotor models, including Diffusion Policy (DP) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, have demonstrated promising performance on robotic manipulation benchmarks. However, their potential remains fundamentally constrained by the scarcity of large-scale embodied trajectory datasets, leading to insufficient compositional generalization in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios with limited capability to capture reusable skill structures. To address this limitation, we propose Skill-Based Memory (SkillMemo) framework that implicitly decomposes long-horizon demonstrations into latent atomic skills and integrates skill-level features into a dynamic episodic memory bank for solving compositional tasks. Specifically, we first introduce an expert-guided trajectory segmentation module built upon a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture, which implicitly partitions trajectories into distinct skill primitives represented by learned gating coefficients. We further design a skill-level episodic memory architecture that stores compact skill representations as retrievable key-value pairs. During inference, the memory bank retrieves the most relevant skill primitives which are subsequently fused with the model's current gating distribution, providing a robust contextual prior to refine action predictions. Extensive experiments on the simulation benchmark and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that SkillMemo consistently enhances both DP and VLA backbones, achieving state-of-the-art performance and outperforming $π_{0.5}$, while exhibiting strong compositional generalization to unseen task configurations.

</details>


### [132] [TRACE: Learned Proprioceptive Odometry for Legged Robots under Unreliable Contact Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05975)
*Taehyeon Kong,Woojin Kim,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一种用于腿式机器人在不可靠接触条件下进行本体感受里程计估计的端到端学习方法。它通过足部感知交叉注意力模块自适应融合IMU和腿部运动学信息，结合物理启发辅助损失与策略随机化提升鲁棒性和sim-to-real迁移能力，在多种地形上相比经典滤波、混合和纯学习方法显著降低位置漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在不规则、滑溜或复杂地形上行走时，接触条件（脚-地接触）往往不可靠，传统的接触阈值或手工定义规则容易失效，导致现有里程计估计方法（滤波、混合、学习）在此类场景下精度显著下降。因此需要一个不依赖显式接触或滑移阈值、能够自适应处理不可靠接触信息的鲁棒本体感受里程计估计器。

Method: TRACE采用端到端架构，从IMU和关节测量的历史序列中预测相对位移、相对旋转和机体坐标系速度。核心设计包括：1) 足部感知交叉注意力模块，自适应地对IMU token和腿部运动学token进行权重分配，无需手工接触/滑移阈值；2) 直接监督加两个物理启发的辅助损失（促进运动学一致性和腿部信息可靠利用）；3) 训练阶段引入策略随机化以缓解策略特定过拟合，并在真实世界中部分微调时间编码器和预测头以提升sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在多种室内外地形上的实验中，TRACE相比经典滤波方法、混合方法以及纯学习方法基线，在不同接触条件下均持续降低了位置漂移。消融实验验证了训练目标、策略随机化和真实世界微调各个组件对不可靠接触和sim-to-real失配场景下性能的贡献。

Conclusion: TRACE证明了通过端到端学习、自适应注意力机制和物理启发式训练约束，可以在不依赖手工接触阈值的前提下显著提升腿式机器人在不可靠接触条件下的里程计精度，同时策略随机化与部分微调策略有效增强了跨仿真到现实的泛化能力。

Abstract: In this paper, we present TRACE (Tokenized Robust Attention for Contact-Aware Estimation), an end-to-end learned proprioceptive odometry estimator for legged robots under unreliable contact conditions. The proposed estimator directly predicts relative displacement, relative rotation, and body-frame velocity from a recent history of onboard inertial and joint measurements. To improve robustness under unreliable contact conditions, we introduce a foot-aware cross-attention module that adaptively weights IMU and leg-wise kinematic tokens without relying on manually defined contact or slip thresholds. The estimator is trained with direct supervision and two physics-inspired auxiliary losses that promote kinematic consistency and reliable use of leg information. To reduce policy-specific overfitting and consequently improve sim-to-real transfer, simulation training incorporates policy randomization, followed by partial real-world fine-tuning of the temporal encoder and prediction head. Experiments across diverse indoor and outdoor terrains demonstrate consistent reductions in position drift compared with classical filtering-based, hybrid, and purely learning-based baselines. Ablation studies further validate the contributions of the proposed training objectives, policy randomization, and real-world fine-tuning, particularly under unreliable contacts and sim-to-real mismatch.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Flat Policies: Hierarchical Post-Training for Embodied Agents in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05999)
*He Kong,Zengjue Chen,Qi Wang,Qianli Xing,Runliang Niu,Peidong Liu,Jiawei Li,Shiqi Wang,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiRoC是一个层级式机器人控制框架，将高层任务规划与低层动作执行解耦，通过强化学习在在线交互中持续提升执行器能力，并通过对齐策略缓解规划与执行之间的分布偏差，在多种机器人操作基准上优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型的后训练方法主要将其优化为扁平策略，难以显式建模任务进度并执行稳健的长时程操作。虽然层级方法引入了任务分解，但主要依赖离线示范的监督学习，无法通过在线交互有效提升执行效果。

Method: HiRoC采用层级后训练框架：高层规划器将复杂任务分解为可执行的子目标以提供显式语义指导；低层执行器通过强化学习持续改进子目标条件下的动作生成。在强化学习之前，先将执行器与规划器生成的子目标进行对齐，以缓解规划与执行之间的分布不匹配。

Result: 在多种机器人操作基准上的大量实验表明，HiRoC一致性地优于强基线方法。综合分析进一步验证了层级后训练的有效性以及每个关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 层级式后训练框架HiRoC有效解决了VLA模型在长时程操作中的任务建模和在线改进问题，通过规划-执行解耦与对齐策略实现了更优的操作性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in robotic manipulation by leveraging pretrained vision-language models. However, existing post-training methods predominantly optimize VLA models as flat policies, making it difficult to explicitly model task progression and perform robust long-horizon manipulation. Although hierarchical approaches introduce task decomposition, they mainly rely on supervised learning from offline demonstrations and cannot effectively improve execution through online interaction. To address this limitation, we propose Hierarchical Robotic Control (HiRoC), a hierarchical post-training framework that decouples high-level task planning from low-level action execution. The planner decomposes complex tasks into executable subgoals to provide explicit semantic guidance, while the executor continuously improves subgoal-conditioned action generation through reinforcement learning. To enable effective collaboration between the two modules, we further align the executor with planner-generated subgoals before reinforcement learning, mitigating the distribution misalignment between planning and execution. Extensive experiments across diverse robotic manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that HiRoC consistently outperforms strong baselines. Comprehensive analyses further validate the effectiveness of hierarchical post-training and the contribution of each key component.

</details>


### [134] [Adaptive-WAM: Quality-Guided Early-Exit Planning from Intermediate Video-Diffusion Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06008)
*Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Adaptive-WAM，一种基于Wan2.2-5B骨干网络的质量感知多出口轨迹规划器。通过观测到规划性能对视频去噪步数和DiT深度不敏感、且中间层即可解码出高质量轨迹，该方法在选定DiT块上附加轨迹扩散头，并用轻量级质量评分器在轨迹满足质量阈值时提前终止推理，避免迭代式无分类器去噪和VAE解码。在NAVSIM上达到90.8 PDMS（自适应）和92.6 PDMS（固定出口+64候选），在nuScenes上零微调迁移获得0.88m平均L2误差和0.08%碰撞率，端到端延迟约170ms，低于固定深度规划的190ms和320ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视频扩散模型为自动驾驶提供了丰富的时空先验，但现有的世界-动作模型即使部署时只需一条自车轨迹，也继承了迭代式未来视频生成的昂贵计算成本。作者提出一个更基本的问题：执行视频扩散模型的多少部分才能做出可靠的驾驶决策？目标是避免不必要的视频生成计算，仅提取决策所需的轨迹信息。

Method: 通过受控实验研究视频去噪时间步和DiT深度对规划性能的影响，发现中间层即可解码出强轨迹。基于此设计Adaptive-WAM：在Wan2.2-5B骨干网络的选定DiT块上附加轨迹扩散头，使用轻量级轨迹质量评分器评估当前解码轨迹，若满足质量阈值则终止推理；否则从缓存的隐藏状态继续计算到更深出口。该规划器完全避免无分类器去噪循环和VAE解码，并根据轨迹质量动态分配骨干网络深度。

Result: 在NAVSIM上，自适应单轨迹规划器达到90.8 PDMS；固定出口变体以64个轨迹候选达到92.6 PDMS。在NAVSIM v2上获得89.9 EPDMS，在对比的前视图视频世界模型规划器中取得最佳结果。无需目标域微调即可迁移至nuScenes，平均L2误差0.88m，碰撞率0.08%。在A100上，自适应路由将PDMS从90.62提升至90.79，平均端到端规划延迟170ms，比固定block-15规划器（190ms）低约10%，比固定全深度规划器（320ms）低47%。

Conclusion: 实验表明，大型视频扩散模型中的许多计算阶段对规划任务而言是冗余的，通过多出口架构和轨迹质量驱动的提前终止机制，可以在几乎不牺牲规划性能（甚至略有提升）的情况下大幅降低推理成本，为视频世界模型在自动驾驶中的高效部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large video diffusion models provide rich spatiotemporal priors for autonomous driving, but existing world-action models often inherit the cost of iterative future-video generation even though deployment only requires an ego trajectory. We ask a more basic question: how much of a video diffusion model must be executed to make a reliable driving decision? Through a controlled study of video denoising timesteps and Diffusion Transformer (DiT) depth, we find that planning performance is largely insensitive to the tested video-noise levels, whereas strong trajectories can already be decoded from intermediate layers. Based on this observation, we introduce Adaptive-WAM, a quality-aware multi-exit planner built on a Wan2.2-5B backbone. Trajectory diffusion heads are attached to selected DiT blocks, and a lightweight trajectory-quality scorer terminates inference once the best trajectory decoded so far satisfies a quality threshold; otherwise, computation continues from the cached hidden state to a deeper exit. The deployed planner therefore avoids the iterative classifier-free denoising loop and VAE decoding required for future-video synthesis, while dynamically allocating backbone depth according to trajectory quality. On NAVSIM, the adaptive single-trajectory planner achieves 90.8 PDMS; a separate fixed-exit variant reaches 92.6 PDMS with 64 proposals. It further obtains 89.9 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, yielding the best reported results among the compared front-view video world-model planners. Without target-domain fine-tuning, Adaptive-WAM transfers to nuScenes with 0.88 m average L2 error and a 0.08\% collision rate. On an A100, adaptive routing improves PDMS from 90.62 to 90.79 while averaging 170 ms end-to-end planning latency, approximately 10\% below the 190 ms fixed block-15 planner and 47\% below the 320 ms fixed full-depth planner. Code will be released.

</details>


### [135] [Topometric Autonomous Vehicle Localization by Combining Visual Embeddings and Feed-Forward 3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06021)
*Eulogio Quemada-Torres,Alberto Jaenal,Francisco-Angel Moreno,Javier Gonzalez-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将视觉位置识别（VPR）与前馈神经三维几何（FF3D）模型相结合的自顶而下（topometric）定位框架，通过离线自动化建图工具建模位姿-外观交互，并利用在线粒子滤波器融合里程计与场景置信度进行FF3D推断，在三个基准上显著优于现有基于外观的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉定位（VL）需要兼具紧凑性、对场景外观变化的鲁棒性和度量精度。VPR通过低维图像嵌入满足前两点，但度量精度不足；而基于局部特征或神经表示的标准VL虽有较高度量精度，但可扩展性和鲁棒性有限。现有方法难以同时满足这些要求，因此需要一种将两者优势整合的框架。

Method: 提出一个topometric框架：1）离线阶段，自动化建图工具建模场景不同区域的位姿-外观交互概率关系；2）在线阶段，粒子滤波器基于里程计和场景置信度估计位姿，并指导FF3D进行度量位姿估计，将神经度量估计融入基于概率外观的定位流程中。框架具有模块化设计，描述子提取器和FF3D模型均可互换。

Result: 在三个公开基准数据集上进行了广泛评估，证明该方法相较于现有基于外观的定位方法有显著改进。进一步的分析表明，顺序化（sequential）的置信度传播能够在感知混淆（perceptual aliasing）情况下缓解严重的定位失败。

Conclusion: 通过将VPR的鲁棒外观识别能力与FF3D的高精度度量估计能力有机结合，并采用topometric框架和粒子滤波进行统一推理，该方法在保持模块化和可扩展性的同时，实现了更优的视觉定位性能，尤其在感知混淆场景下具有更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Effective Visual Localization (VL) requires a map of the environment that combines compactness for efficient scalability with robustness against visual appearance changes and metric precision. Through low-dimensional image embeddings, Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is able to successfully meet the first two requirements, but its low metric accuracy makes it less suitable than standard VL approaches based on local features or neural representations. This limitation can be overcome by integrating VPR with the accurate local trajectory estimates produced by feed-forward neural 3D geometry (FF3D) models. In this paper, we address sequential appearance-based localization through a topometric framework that iteratively combines probabilistic VPR with FF3D metric pose estimation in controlled image sets. Our approach proposes an automatic offline mapping tool that models the topometric pose-appearance interaction in the different parts of the scene.
