This map is later employed by an online particle filter that estimates the pose from odometry and belief over places for FF3D inference, successfully incorporating neural metric estimation into probabilistic appearance-based localization. We extensively evaluate the framework on three known benchmarks, demonstrating substantial improvements over existing appearance-based methods. The modularity of our approach allows the descriptor extractor and FF3D model to remain interchangeable, and a focused analysis further shows that sequential belief can mitigate severe failures under perceptual aliasing.

</details>


### [136] [IcFuzz: Fuzzing Isaac Sim with Semantic Stage Guidance and Multi-level Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06088)
*Zhixiang Chen,Zhuangbin Chen,Ruoxi Jia,Zeqin Liao,Wei Li,Jinyang Liu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IcFuzz是首个针对NVIDIA Isaac Sim模拟器的模糊测试方法，通过LLM语义阶段分割、多层级变异算子和多臂老虎机调度策略，在代码覆盖率和缺陷检测上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Isaac Sim作为GPU加速的机器人模拟器，结构复杂且存在软件缺陷，但现有模糊测试方法无法有效应对其上下文感知的对象语义、层级化仿真控制和庞大的仿真状态空间等挑战。

Method: IcFuzz首先利用LLM进行语义阶段分割，将仿真程序分解为结构化阶段以捕获上下文感知的对象语义；基于此设计多层级变异算子来系统性地测试不同粒度层次的模拟器；最后采用多臂老虎机算法自适应地调度变异算子，以高效探索庞大的仿真状态空间。

Result: 实验结果显示IcFuzz在代码覆盖率和缺陷检测方面均优于基线方法，达到基线约190%-205%的代码覆盖率，在12小时测试中平均检测到3.7个独特崩溃（基线为0），并在约四个月内发现11个缺陷，其中9个已获开发者确认或修复。

Conclusion: IcFuzz证明了将大语言模型与自适应调度策略相结合可以有效解决复杂机器人模拟器的模糊测试难题，为嵌入式AI系统基础设施的可靠性验证提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Robotics simulators serve as a foundational infrastructure for embodied AI, facilitating safe and scalable robotic system development. NVIDIA Isaac Sim has emerged as one of the most popular simulators, distinguished by its GPU-accelerated physics engine and photorealistic rendering, which enable high-fidelity modeling of complex environments. However, its inherent complexity inevitably introduces software bugs that can compromise simulation reliability. Existing fuzzing approaches struggle to test Isaac Sim effectively due to challenges of context-aware object semantics, hierarchical simulation control, and a vast simulation state space.
  In this paper, we propose IcFuzz, the first fuzzing approach for Isaac Sim. IcFuzz first performs an LLM-based semantic stage segmentation, decomposing simulation programs into structured stages that capture context-aware object semantics. Guided by this information, IcFuzz designs multi-level mutation operators to systematically exercise the simulator across hierarchical granularities. To efficiently navigate the vast simulation state space, IcFuzz employs a multi-armed bandit algorithm to adaptively schedule mutation operators. Experimental results show that IcFuzz outperforms the baselines in terms of both code coverage and bug detection. Specifically, IcFuzz achieves approximately 190\%--205\% of the code coverage of the baselines and detects an average of 3.7 unique crashes over three rounds of 12-hour tests, while no crashes are detected by the baselines. Moreover, IcFuzz has uncovered 11 bugs over approximately four months, 9 of which have been confirmed or fixed by the developers.

</details>


### [137] [Visual Grounding in Zero-Shot Vision-Language Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06154)
*J. de Curtò,Dayani Plasencia,Diego Sánchez,I. de Zarzà*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过输入消融测试（盲图控制、重复输入、镜像反射等）系统评估了视觉语言模型（VLM）作为零样本控制器的感知接地能力。结果显示直接控制表现普遍不佳——恒定SLOW策略甚至优于脚本化几何控制器，多个模型对图像不敏感，没有任何本地VLM同时满足纵向和横向接地标准。然而，一个仅依赖图像的确定性正控制模型能以0.090m MAE估算前车间距并具备精确镜像对称性，证明失败是模块化的而非普遍的。研究者进一步提出一个防泄漏的对称一致性守护机制，从16帧校准中选择两个模型并冻结2/4危险投票，在272帧上达到0.954平衡准确率（95% CI [0.895,0.990]），并在确定性感知保留横向权威的架构下实现0.934动作一致性和精确镜像等变。结论表明当前VLM应作为有边界、选择性的危险辅助工具，而非全能的零样本控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于：VLM被广泛用作零样本控制器，但成功的轨迹并不必然证明决策真正接地于视觉输入——模拟器动力学和保守动作先验可能仅凭幸运就产生可观分数。作者希望区分"视觉接地"与"非视觉捷径"，揭示VLM感知能力的真实边界，并探明其失效是模块性的还是系统性的。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 输入消融电池：盲图控制、重复相同输入、车道轴反射、非视觉基线和流水线完整性检查；(2) 对9个直接动作模型、6个结构化本地VLM和一个探索性VLM-MPC层级进行32,874次评分调用分析，覆盖两个本体和三个模拟器；(3) 提出后验的防泄漏对称一致性守护（symmetry-consensus guardian），从16帧校准帧中选出两个模型，冻结2-of-4危险投票，并在272帧保留集上验证；(4) 采用确定性感知保留横向权威的离线模块化重放架构。

Result: 直接控制结果大体为负面：恒定SLOW策略胜过脚本化几何控制器；多个模型图像不变或近乎恒定；能识别纵向危险的模型在镜像反射下仍无法转换LEFT/RIGHT；无本地VLM同时满足纵向和横向接地标准。但图像纯确定性正控制以0.090m MAE估计前车间距并具备精确镜像等变，证明刺激携带的视觉信息充分。所提守护机制在272帧上达到0.954平衡准确率（bootstrap 95% CI [0.895,0.990]），嵌套留一episode交叉验证在全部12折中恢复相同模型对和阈值；弃权机制将承诺平衡准确率提升至0.973（覆盖率0.824）；离线模块化重放达到0.934动作一致性和精确镜像等变。

Conclusion: 结论是：当前VLM不适合作为整体零样本控制器，但可以作为有边界、选择性的危险辅助（hazard assistant）——即在特定模态和特定场景下提供有意义的预警信号，同时需要确定性感知模块保留横向控制权威。这为VLM在安全关键驾驶/机器人控制中的实际部署提供了分层架构指导。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as zero-shot controllers, but successful trajectories do not necessarily show that decisions are grounded in visual input: simulator dynamics and conservative action priors can produce favourable scores without meaningful perception. We investigate this with an input-ablation battery: blind-image controls, repeated identical inputs, lane-axis reflection, non-visual baselines, and pipeline-integrity checks. Across nine direct-action models, six structured local VLMs, and an exploratory VLM-MPC hierarchy, we analyse 32,874 scored calls over two embodiments and three simulators. The direct-control results are largely negative: a constant-SLOW policy outperforms a scripted geometric controller, several models are image-invariant or nearly constant, and models that recognize longitudinal hazards still fail to transform LEFT and RIGHT under reflection. No local VLM meets the joint longitudinal and lateral grounding criteria. However, an image-only deterministic positive control estimates the lead gap with 0.090 m MAE and exact mirror equivariance, confirming the stimuli carry sufficient visual information; the failures are modular, not universal. A post-hoc, leakage-controlled symmetry-consensus guardian selects two models from 16 calibration frames and freezes a 2-of-4 hazard vote across original and reflected views. On 272 held-out frames it reaches 0.954 balanced accuracy (episode-cluster bootstrap 95% CI [0.895,0.990]); nested leave-one-episode-out recovers the same pair and threshold in all 12 folds. Abstaining on ties raises committed balanced accuracy to 0.973 at 0.824 coverage. With deterministic perception retaining lateral authority, offline modular replay achieves 0.934 action agreement and exact mirror equivariance. These results support current VLMs as bounded, selective hazard assistants, not monolithic zero-shot controllers.

</details>


### [138] [Prior-SG: Task and Prior Driven Region Segmentation for Scene Graphs in Arbitrarily-Structured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06170)
*Giorgio Tonetti,Laurent Kneip,Abel Gawel,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Prior-SG，一个任务与先验驱动的3D场景图生成框架，将场景图生成视为概率对齐问题，通过马尔可夫随机场融合多模态特征与LLM生成的拓扑先验，实现开放平面环境中的高精度语义区域分割与零样本本体论灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D场景图提取方法依赖局部视觉聚类或严格的几何启发式（如墙体分隔房间），在开放平面或任意结构环境中表现不佳，无法实现高层级空间推理中的鲁棒语义理解。

Method: Prior-SG包含三个核心组件：(1) 多尺度开放词汇特征融合策略，将RGB-D传感器流聚合为物理锚定的实例图；(2) 利用大型语言模型动态合成环境结构逻辑期望和任务相关词汇的先验图；(3) 通过最大化后验估计优化马尔可夫随机场，融合视觉、几何和离散对象等多源异质专家信息与拓扑先验，解决局部感知歧义。

Result: 在多种模拟住宅数据集和大型开放平面真实环境中验证，Prior-SG在语义区域分割准确性上达到最先进水平，能在无物理墙的情况下鲁棒划定远距离功能边界，并具备零样本本体论灵活性，可根据高层任务重新构建空间划分。

Conclusion: Prior-SG通过将任务先验与多模态感知融合，克服了几何启发式的局限，实现了开放环境下更灵活、更语义化的场景图构建，为自主移动平台的高层空间推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D scene graphs are a promising representation for high-level spatial reasoning in autonomous mobile platforms. However, existing extraction frameworks typically rely on purely local visual clustering or strict geometric heuristics, such as wall-separated rooms, which fail in open-plan or arbitrarily-structured environments. We propose Prior-SG, a task- and prior-driven framework that casts scene graph generation fundamentally as a probabilistic alignment problem. As the robot explores, it continuously aggregates an incoming RGB-D sensor stream into a physically grounded Instance Graph utilizing a multi-scale, open-vocabulary feature fusion strategy. The system then infers the high-level functional semantics of this map through a Maximum A Posteriori (MAP) estimate, guided by a Prior Graph-a logical expectation of the environment's structure and task-relevant vocabulary synthesized dynamically by a Large Language Model. By optimizing a Markov Random Field that fuses heterogeneous experts (visual, geometric, and discrete objects) with these topological priors, the system resolves local perceptual ambiguities. We validate this approach across diverse simulated residential datasets and large, open-plan real-world environments. Prior-SG achieves state-of-the-art semantic region segmentation accuracy compared to recent baselines, robustly delineates distant functional boundaries in the absence of physical walls, and uniquely provides zero-shot ontological flexibility, enabling the robot to entirely restructure its spatial partitioning based on a given high-level task.

</details>


### [139] [ErgoSurf: Ergodic Control for the Coverage of Unknown Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06208)
*Stefan Schneyer,Timo Bachmann,Maged Iskandar,Korbinian Nottensteiner,Alin Albu-Schäffer,Freek Stulp,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于触觉感知的在线遍历控制框架，在未知表面上同时实现系统覆盖与几何重建，通过GPIS模型学习表面几何，利用热扩散类比计算势场引导机器人遍历探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遍历控制方法在表面接触类任务（如检测、清洁、打磨）中依赖先验表面几何知识或视觉传感器预扫描，无法应对未知或动态环境，限制了实际应用场景。

Method: 采用高斯过程隐式表面（GPIS）模型从内在触觉传感中在线学习全局表面几何；利用接触点处切平面采样的点云局部近似表面，并迭代拟合高斯过程；将点云同时作为目标分布与覆盖分布的采样域；通过热扩散类比计算势场，将空间覆盖目标转化为平滑的机器人轨迹。

Result: 通过仿真和真实机器人实验验证了框架的有效性，实现了同步的遍历覆盖与在线表面几何学习，重建误差接近真实地面真值。

Conclusion: 该框架消除了对表面几何先验知识的依赖，仅凭触觉感知即可在未知表面上实现遍历覆盖与几何重建，拓展了接触类任务在未知环境中的适用性。

Abstract: Contact-centric tasks on surfaces, ranging from inspection and cleaning to sanding and polishing, require robots to systematically cover the surface while maintaining stable contact. Ergodic control generates trajectories that spend time at a location proportional to a desired, task-specific spatial distribution, enabling efficient information gathering and coverage. However, traditional ergodic control methods rely on prior knowledge of surface geometry or require a vision sensory input to scan the geometry beforehand, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios with unknown or dynamic environments. This paper introduces a novel online ergodic control framework that achieves systematic surface coverage while simultaneously reconstructing unknown surface geometry. We employ a Gaussian Process Implicit Surface (GPIS) model that learns global surface geometry from intrinsic tactile sensing during execution. For efficient online planning, we approximate the surface locally using point clouds sampled from tangent planes at observed contact points and iteratively fit them to the Gaussian Process. This approximation simultaneously serves as the sampling domain for both the target and the coverage distributions. We employ a heat-diffusion analogy to compute potential fields that guide ergodic exploration, translating spatial coverage objectives into smooth robot trajectories. We demonstrate our framework through simulation and real-robot experiments, validating simultaneous ergodic coverage and online surface geometry learning with reconstruction errors approaching the ground truth.

</details>


### [140] [VIDP: Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy for Compliant Robot Manipulation from Diverse Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06210)
*Hisham Khalil,Neil Fernandes,Thomas M. Kwok,Hsiu-Chin Lin,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VIDP框架，一种基于模仿学习的可变阻抗控制方法，通过任务参数化的方向感知混合模型从多样化演示中提取物理一致的轨迹分布，并将其映射为刚度分布，使机器人能够在无力传感器的情况下联合预测位姿动作和任务柔性，在接触丰富的操作任务中显著优于固定阻抗基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作需要精确跟踪与机械柔性的兼顾，静态阻抗无法适应变化的接触约束。虽然可变阻抗技能可从演示中学习以避免复杂建模，但柔性是力不可知运动数据中的隐藏变量，现有方法从轨迹变化中推断柔性时，轨迹变化可能反映几何适应而非有意柔性，尤其是在空间布局变化时。

Method: 提出可变阻抗扩散策略（VIDP），包含两个核心组件：1）任务参数化方向感知混合模型（TP-DAMM），从多样演示中提取物理一致的轨迹分布；2）将提取的分布映射为刚度曲线，使策略能联合预测位姿动作和任务依从性，全程无需力传感器。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，VIDP在任务成功率上显著优于固定阻抗基线；相对于高刚度控制器减少了交互力，相对于低刚度基线降低了跟踪误差，实现了鲁棒性与柔顺性的良好平衡。

Conclusion: 通过将轨迹分布映射到刚度曲线，VIDP成功地在无外力传感器条件下实现了可变阻抗控制，验证了从运动学演示中提取任务依从性的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires precise tracking and mechanical compliance, where variable impedance control can improve robustness in task success, whereas static compliance cannot adapt to varying contact constraints. Variable impedance skills can be learned from demonstrations, avoiding complex modeling, but compliance is a hidden variable in force-agnostic kinematic data. While existing methods infer compliance from trajectory variations, these variations may reflect geometric adaptation and not intentional compliance when subject to changing spatial layouts. Therefore, this letter introduces Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy (VIDP), an imitation learning-based variable impedance control framework leveraging a Task-Parameterized Directionality-Aware Mixture Model (TP-DAMM) to extract physically consistent trajectory distributions from diverse demonstrations. By mapping distributions to stiffness profiles, VIDP jointly predicts pose actions and task compliance without force sensors. Real-world experiments show that VIDP significantly outperforms fixed-impedance baselines in task success rate while reducing interaction forces with respect to high stiffness controllers and tracking errors with respect to low stiffness baselines.

</details>


### [141] [Design and Evaluation of a Touchscreen-Based Teleoperation Interface for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06219)
*Juan José García Cárdenas,Alperen Kenan,Hamidreza Raei,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani,Arash Ajoudani,Adriana Tapus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并评估了一种新型触屏遥操作界面，将手指连续动作直接映射到机械臂运动，用于核工业表面接触任务（如拭子采样）。与传统的操纵杆界面相比，触屏界面在任务效率、精确性和认知负荷方面均表现更优：完成时间减少53.5%，路径覆盖率更高，NASA-TLX认知负荷降低17.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核工业中的表面接触任务（如拭子采样）需要精确的路径和力跟踪、避障以及持续的操作员注意力，传统操纵杆界面难以有效支持此类精细任务。开发更直观、更高效的遥操作界面对于提高核工业中机器人操作的安全性和有效性至关重要。

Method: 设计了一种将连续手指运动直接映射为机械臂运动的触屏遥操作界面，提供更精细的速度控制，并将控制与可视化集成。采用Franka Emika Panda机械臂开展跨国的远程遥操作试验，招募20名参与者进行对比用户研究，比较触屏、传统操纵杆和单键自主三种控制模式。通过运动学数据、生理数据和行为数据综合评估任务性能、认知负荷（NASA-TLX）和操作员信任度。

Result: 触屏界面显著优于操纵杆：完成时间中位数从5.38分钟降至2.50分钟（减少53.5%），正弦路径覆盖率从84.1%提升至90.7%，两种路径几何上的过冲量均更低。NASA-TLX认知负荷从操纵杆的52降至触屏的43（-17.3%），自主模式最低为31。触屏界面在效率和准确性上均表现最佳。

Conclusion: 该触屏遥操作界面易于实现，在遥操作表面任务中能有效提升任务性能并降低操作员认知负荷，为核工业等挑战性环境中的机器人遥操作提供了一种更自然、直观且实用的交互方案。

Abstract: Intuitive teleoperation interfaces are crucial for the safe and effective operation of robotic manipulators in challenging environments. In the nuclear industry, surface contact tasks such as swab sampling require precise path and force tracking, obstacle avoidance, and sustained operator attention, which conventional joystick interfaces struggle to support effectively.
  This study designs and evaluates a novel touchscreen teleoperation interface that maps continuous finger movements directly to robotic manipulator motions, provides finer velocity control, and integrates control with visualization, enabling more natural, precise, and intuitive surface interaction than conventional controllers. A comparative user study with 20 participants evaluated task performance and workload using the proposed touchscreen, a conventional joystick, and a single-click autonomous mode. Tasks simulated realistic surface manipulation using a Franka Emika Panda arm, remotely controlled from another country. Kinematic, physiological, and behavioral data were recorded to comprehensively assess task performance, cognitive load, and operator trust across each control condition.
  Participants completed teleoperation tasks more efficiently and accurately with the touchscreen interface, achieving a 53.5% reduction in completion time (median: 2.50 vs. 5.38 min), higher in-area coverage on the sinusoidal path (90.7% vs. 84.1%), and lower overshoot on both path geometries compared with the joystick. Cognitive load, quantified via NASA-TLX (0-100), decreased from joystick to touchscreen (mean TLX 52 to 43; -9 points, -17.3%) and was lowest under the autonomous one-click mode (31; -21 points vs. joystick, -40.4%; -12 vs. touchscreen, -27.9%). This research presents an easy-to-implement touchscreen interface that improves performance in teleoperated surface tasks while reducing cognitive load.

</details>


### [142] [Robot Learning from Human Demonstrations: Handwritten Alphabet Trajectories and Human-Likeness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06221)
*Alperen Kenan,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从演示中学习类人机器人运动的完整框架，包括数据采集、概率轨迹学习和感知用户评估，通过融合力与归一化时间维度扩展了GMM/GMR方法，并处理多段非连续轨迹。基于22名参与者绘制的3,142个手写字母演示数据集，生成的轨迹在用户研究中获得71.50/100的类人评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从演示学习（LfD）使机器人无需显式编程即可通过观察和模仿人类动力学发展运动技能，产生的类人机器人运动被视为人机交互中建立信任和实现自然协作的关键因素。现有方法在表征人类动力学丰富信息（力、时间）和处理非连续多段轨迹方面存在不足，需要一个完整且可复现的框架来学习并评估类人机器人运动。

Method: 框架包括三个部分：（1）通过触摸屏遥操作界面采集22名参与者的3,142个手写演示，覆盖52种拉丁字母大小写组合，捕获平面位置、接触力和时序；（2）在GMM/GMR基础上引入力和归一化时间维度以丰富人类动力学表征，并适配非连续多段轨迹以实现跨演示泛化；（3）通过21人的用户研究，采用0-100连续量表评估生成轨迹的类人感知度，50为中性中点。

Result: 生成的轨迹整体类人评分为71.50（SD=22.56），表明多数轨迹被感知为更接近人类。参与者认为几何定位和轨迹序列是最有影响力的感知因素，并对类人机器人行为持积极态度。数据集已开源发布。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了机器人运动的类人感知特性，通过纳入力与时间维度以及处理多段轨迹的能力，为基于演示的类人机器人运动学习提供了可复现的基准。开源数据集为后续研究提供了标准化评估基础。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) provides a developmental framework through which robots can develop motor skills by observing and imitating human dynamics, reducing reliance on explicit programming to teach a skill to a robot. The resulting human-like robot motion is recognised as a key factor in building trust and enabling natural collaboration in human-robot interaction. This paper presents a framework for learning human-like robot motion from demonstration, including data collection, probabilistic trajectory learning, and perceptual user evaluation. A dataset of 3,142 handwriting demonstrations was collected from 22 participants across all 52 Latin alphabet character-case combinations via a touchscreen teleoperation interface, capturing planar position, contact force, and timing. Building on the widely used Gaussian Mixture Model and Gaussian Mixture Regression approach for learning from demonstration, the framework is extended in this work by incorporating force and normalised time dimensions to enable richer representation of human dynamics, and adapting it to handle non-continuous, multi-segment trajectories, enabling generalisation across demonstrations. A user study with 21 participants evaluated the perceived human-likeness of the generated trajectories using a continuous scale anchored between robotic and human-like motion, normalised to 0-100 where 50 represents the neutral midpoint. The generated trajectories achieved an overall human-likeness score of 71.50 (SD=22.56), indicating that the majority of trajectories were perceived as more human-like. Participants identified geometric positioning and trajectory sequence as the most influential perceptual factors, and reported positive attitudes toward human-like robot behaviour. The datasets are released as open-source, providing a reproducible benchmark for developing and evaluating human-like robot motion methods.

</details>


### [143] [GeniWorld: A Generalizable Interactive World Model for Robotic Manipulation via Visual Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06332)
*Chenghao Gu,Hanyang Yu,Jingbo Zhang,Haitao Lin,Wenyao Zhang,Jinghe Wang,Hanglei Jin,Shuzhao Xie,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeniWorld是一个基于预训练视频生成模型的交互式世界模型，通过URDF渲染将数值动作转化为视觉动作表征，解耦本体运动学与环境动态，实现跨未见场景的鲁棒泛化，并支持闭环控制、策略评估和轨迹生成等下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在复杂和未见环境中鲁棒性有限，在多样真实环境中扩展机器人学习和评估昂贵且困难。现有动作条件世界模型存在动作可控性差、对分布外场景泛化能力不足的问题，亟需一种能够在未见场景中鲁棒泛化的交互式世界模型。

Method: 基于预训练视频生成模型构建世界模型；使用URDF渲染将数值动作转化为可视化动作表征，实现空间上的动作控制；显式解耦本体运动学与环境动态，缓解场景过拟合并促进机器人-环境交互建模；构造自回归视频预测模型并与高频机器人运动控制集成，实现闭环控制，支持机器人策略和人工遥操作。

Result: 即使在仅有有限固定场景数据训练的情况下，模型在域内性能上表现优越，并在高度随机化的未见环境中实现鲁棒的零样本泛化。作为策略评估器在环境扰动下保持可靠；同时仅需有限的真实演示数据即可在世界模型中生成多样化的操作轨迹，提升下游策略在复杂环境中的性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GeniWorld展示了将预训练视频生成模型与URDF运动学解耦相结合的有效性，为机器人世界模型在数据稀缺条件下实现跨场景零样本泛化提供了可行路径，可作为可扩展的策略评估器和数据增强生成器，提升下游机器人策略的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies exhibit strong capabilities, but their robustness in complex and unseen environments remains limited. Scaling robot learning and evaluation in diverse real-world environments remains costly and challenging. Action-conditioned world models offer a promising alternative, but they often suffer from limited action controllability and poor generalization to out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To this end, we present GeniWorld, an interactive world model for robots that generalizes robustly across unseen scenarios. Building on pretrained video generative models, we use URDF-based rendering to transform numerical actions into visual action representations, enabling spatially grounded action control. By explicitly decoupling embodiment kinematics from environmental dynamics, our model mitigates scene overfitting and facilitates modeling of robot-environment interactions. To achieve closed-loop control, we construct an autoregressive video prediction model integrated with high-frequency robot kinematic control, enabling interaction with both robot policies and human teleoperators. In our experiments, even when trained solely on limited fixed-scene data, our model achieves superior in-domain performance and robust zero-shot generalization to highly randomized, unseen environments. For downstream applications, GeniWorld serves as a scalable policy evaluator that remains reliable under environmental perturbations. Furthermore, even with limited real-world demonstrations, GeniWorld generates diverse manipulation trajectories within the world model, improving downstream policy performance and robustness in complex environments.

</details>


### [144] [A Master-Salve Robot Manipulator for Needle-Based Teleoperation in MRI Chamber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06354)
*Omar Curiel,Jing-Yuan Huang,Po-Chih Chen,Ji Ma,Qing Dai,Wenqi Zhou,David Lu,Holden H. Wu,Tsu-Chin Tsao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于MRI手术室腹部长入操作的MR安全主从机器人操作器，通过流体传动实现2+1自由度主从运动与力传递，并集成了数字主控制器以支持手动、数字、混合及协同控制模式，初步MRI扫描仪实验验证了床边手动控制模式下体内猪实验的功能可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI引导的介入手术需要在强磁场环境下进行，传统电机驱动手术机器人无法在扫描仪内使用。现有研究中的主从或混合控制方案通常采用分轴或模式切换的简化配置，缺乏多模式协同控制能力，且难以实现高透明度力反馈和精密运动传递。因此需要开发一种MRI安全的、具备多种控制模式且传动性能优越的机器人操作器。

Method: 采用2+1自由度主从结构：主控制器由人手操作，通过流体传动将运动和力传递给从端操作器。系统中采用两类驱动机制：高输入阻抗、低泄漏的弹性体流体驱动器用于远端角度控制；低摩擦石墨活塞气缸用于针插入轴的远程驱动，以实现在床边流体管道长度上的亚牛顿力透明度和亚毫米运动传递。此外，数字主控制器提供超越传统分轴或模式切换混合人机控制器的多模态控制能力，支持手动、数字、混合及协同控制四种模式。

Result: 初步MRI扫描仪实验结果表明，该操作器在床边手动控制模式下可支持体内猪实验，验证了其功能可行性。协同控制任务如辅助组织穿透、故障驱动的虚拟夹具以及通过反馈控制实现的运动补偿均在文中展示。

Conclusion: 该MR安全主从机器人操作器通过创新的流体驱动设计和多模态数字控制器架构，实现了MRI实时引导下多种控制模式（手动、数字、混合、协同）的介入手术支持，为MRI环境下的精密介入操作提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We present a MR safe, master-slave robot manipulator for abdominal interventions in the MRI chamber. A human operated 2+1-DoF master controller manipulator transmits motion and force to a 2+1-DoF slave manipulator via fluid transmission. Jointly, a digital master controller provides multimodal control capability beyond common split axis or mode switchable hybrid human-digital controller configurations found in previous studies. High input impedance, low-leakage, elastomeric fluid actuators are delegated to remote angulation control. Low-friction graphite piston cylinders are delegated to needle insertion axis remote actuation given the sub-newton force transparency and sub-millimeter motion transmission over bedside fluid piping lengths. The device enables real-time MRI guided interventions allowing manual, digital, hybrid, and collaborative control modes. Collaborative tasks such as assisted tissue penetration, fault-driven virtual fixture, and motion compensation through feedback control are presented in this paper. Preliminary MR scanner results demonstrate manipulator functional viability for an in-vivo pig experiment in bedside, manual control mode configuration.

</details>


### [145] [DyPES-VLA: Learning Shared Dynamics Priors and Embodiment-Specific Control for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06374)
*Junfeng Li,Junjie He,Zhide Zhong,Yangyang Zheng,Pingyue Sheng,Jiayu Dong,Ruixin Li,Haodong Yan,Jiaguan Zhu,Tianran Zhang,Runze Yu,Wen Chen,Liuqing Yang,Yuxiang Gao,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DyPES-VLA是一种跨本体（cross-embodiment）的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过学习共享动力学先验（Dynamics Priors）和本体特定控制（Embodiment-Specific control）来解决异构机器人本体的泛化问题，在LIBERO、RoboCasa-GR1和RoboTwin 2.0基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨本体VLA方法存在两个主要局限：一是未能充分利用跨不同视觉和交互数据中共享的动力学先验，限制了跨本体迁移能力；二是需要大量人工预处理将本体特定的动作转换为统一格式，增加了部署成本。

Method: 该工作提出两个核心设计：(1) 通过在未来预测目标上训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）来学习共享动力学先验，驱使其共享查询表示捕获物体运动、接触和交互引发的场景变化；(2) 设计本体特定的混合专家（MoE）动作头，直接在各自原生动作空间中生成可执行控制，无需人工预先对齐异构动作。该动作头共享注意力层以捕获时序动作结构共性，同时使用本体特定的前馈专家来处理不同本体的运动学约束和控制语义。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界评估中均达到SOTA性能：LIBERO上取得98.0%的成功率，RoboCasa-GR1上取得59.25%，RoboTwin 2.0上取得89.02%。

Conclusion: DyPES-VLA证明通过将共享动力学先验学习与本体特定控制解耦，可以有效解决异构机器人本体的通用策略训练问题，避免了繁琐的手动动作对齐过程，同时实现了更强的跨本体迁移能力和整体性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a powerful paradigm for robot manipulation, but training a single generalist policy for heterogeneous robot embodiments remains an open problem. Existing methods have two main limitations. First, they underuse dynamics priors shared across diverse visual and interaction data, limiting cross-embodiment transfer. Second, they require extensive manual preprocessing to convert embodiment-specific actions into a common format. To overcome these limitations, we propose DyPES-VLA, a cross-embodiment VLA that learns shared Dynamics Priors and Embodiment-Specific control. First, we learn shared dynamics priors by training the vision-language model (VLM) with a future-prediction objective on cross-embodiment data, driving the shared query representation to capture object motion, contact, and interaction-induced scene changes. Second, an embodiment-specific Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) action head translates these shared dynamics priors into executable controls directly in each embodiment's native action space, without manually pre-aligning heterogeneous actions into a common format. This head shares attention layers to capture common temporal action structures, while its embodiment-specific feed-forward experts resolve the unique kinematic constraints and control semantics of distinct embodiments. As a generalist policy, our \ourmethod achieves state-of-the-art performance across simulation and real-world evaluations, reaching 98.0% success on LIBERO, 59.25% on RoboCasa-GR1, and 89.02% on RoboTwin~2.0.

</details>


### [146] [$ω$-0: A Latent Predictive World Action Model for Concurrent Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06375)
*Zhe Li,Zhenzhe Zhang,Yangyang Wei,Wenjie Zhang,Xichen Yuan,Peiyuan Zhi,Gen Li,Xinying Guo,Fengjie Gao,Jianfei Yang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ω-0，一种用于真实世界人形机器人并发移动-操作任务的潜在预测全身世界动作模型。该模型接收语言指令、视觉观察和本体感受状态，直接预测控制器兼容的全身动作潜变量，通过轻量级预测目标（紧凑的未来观察嵌入）耦合潜在视觉前瞻与基于扩散的全身动作生成，支持多种视觉输入模态。作者还收集了40+小时的真实世界家庭人形数据集ω-HOME，并在11项家庭任务上验证了模型优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在完成家庭任务时通常需要同时进行移动和操作（并发locomotion-manipulation），要求机器人协调地移动、调整姿态、保持平衡并操作物体。然而现有的人形机器人策略通常将移动和操作解耦处理，而近期的工作动作模型要么以手臂为中心（arm-centric），要么以视频为中心（video-centered），缺乏对全身协调并发移动-操作任务的完整建模。

Method: ω-0是一个潜在预测的全身世界动作模型。其核心设计包括：（1）不重建未来视频，而是学习紧凑的未来观察嵌入作为轻量级预测目标，将潜在视觉前瞻与基于扩散的全身动作生成耦合；（2）直接预测控制器兼容的全身动作潜变量供真实机器人执行；（3）支持第一人称RGB、第三人称RGB和第三人称深度三种视觉输入；（4）利用基于控制器的仿真回放将人类/公开视觉-运动先验落地为机器人可执行的动作潜变量。同时收集了40+小时的ω-HOME真实世界家庭数据集，包含多视角观察、全身SMPL动作、机器人状态和动作潜变量。

Result: 在11项真实世界家庭任务上的实验表明，单一的ω-0模型能够生成平滑的"边移动边操作"行为，并一致性地优于代表性模仿学习、VLA（视觉-语言-动作）、人形机器人和WAM（世界动作模型）基线。

Conclusion: ω-0通过潜在预测范式有效地解决了人形机器人并发locomotion-manipulation的挑战，验证了不依赖视频重建的潜在世界模型在人形全身协调控制中的有效性，并为真实世界人形家务操作提供了可行的数据与模型方案。

Abstract: Humanoid household tasks often require concurrent loco-manipulation, where the robot must move, adjust posture, maintain balance, and manipulate objects as a single coordinated behavior. Yet existing humanoid policies typically decompose locomotion and manipulation, while recent world-action models remain either arm-centric or video-centered. We present $ω$-0, a latent predictive whole-body world-action model for real-world humanoid concurrent loco-manipulation. Given a language instruction, current visual observation, and robot proprioceptive state, $ω$-0 directly predicts controller-compatible whole-body action latents for real-robot execution. Rather than reconstructing future videos, $ω$-0 learns compact future observation embeddings as a lightweight predictive objective, coupling latent visual foresight with diffusion-based whole-body action generation. The model supports egocentric RGB, exocentric RGB, and exocentric depth inputs, and leverages controller-based simulation replay to ground human/public visual-motion priors into robot-executable action latents. We further collect $ω$-HOME, a 40+ hour real-world household humanoid dataset with synchronized multi-view observations, whole-body SMPL motions, robot states, and action latents. Real-world experiments on 11 household tasks demonstrate that a single $ω$-0 model can produce smooth manipulate-while-moving behaviors and consistently outperform representative imitation learning, VLA, humanoid, and WAM baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Nesting，一种多智能体协作框架，将现有企业应用封装为自主AI智能体并组织成层级嵌套结构，通过自然语言交互和中央编排器实现跨应用查询与流程编排，提出了"Application-as-Agent"集成范式和"Conversation-as-Integration"交互理念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业运营依赖多个异构业务系统和信息应用，导致严重的数据孤岛和流程碎片化。传统集成方案（ESB、API网关、RPA）存在架构耦合度高、运维成本攀升和智能能力受限等固有缺陷，难以有效编排和利用已投入巨资构建的企业应用。

Method: 提出Agentic Nesting框架：1）将现有企业应用封装为层级嵌套结构中的自主AI智能体，形成与真实企业生态复杂度对应的分层管理模式；2）从每个遗留应用中提取数字代理（digital agent proxy），支持自然语言交互和自主操作；3）通过中央编排器协调多个智能体，实现任务分解与动态调度；4）提供统一对话界面用于跨应用查询和流程编排。

Result: 论文的主要贡献包括：提出"Application-as-Agent"集成范式与"Conversation-as-Integration"交互理念，并探索了该方法论在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用场景中的泛化潜力。具体实验评估结果在摘要中未详细报告。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting通过将企业应用智能体化和层级化组织，为克服传统企业应用集成方式的局限性提供了新的路径，以对话式交互替代了高耦合的中间件架构，有望在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用中展现更强的适配性和扩展性。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [148] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Ignition Index (I)的新指标，通过拟合四参数S形曲线到逐层线性探针准确率上，量化了Transformer语言模型中符合全局工作空间理论（GWT）的全或无点火预测。在11个模型上验证了其9.6倍的选择性，并发现前馈Transformer优于SSM架构、循环架构沿迭代轴表现出工作空间样转换、训练过程中存在相位转变等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局工作空间理论（GWT）预测认知加工中存在全或无的点火现象，但此前缺乏一个经过验证的标量指标来在Transformer语言模型中量化这一动态预测，也缺少连接GWT动力学预测与机制可解释性的定量桥梁。

Method: 提出Ignition Index (I)：对每层线性探针准确率随输入信号强度的变化拟合四参数S形曲线，提取陡峭度参数beta-hat作为点火指标；使用洗牌标签作为控制条件验证选择性；在11个模型（覆盖5个架构家族）上进行统计比较，并使用Mann-Whitney U检验、Cohen's d、PELT断点检测等方法分析结果。

Result: 洗牌标签控制显示9.6倍选择性（p<0.001）；前馈Transformer比SSM在聚合beta-hat上高89%（p<1e-13，Cohen's d=0.52）；Huginn-3.5B沿迭代轴的点火比深度轴高2.12倍；Pythia-410M在训练步骤256处检测到相位转变（+67%）；点火与模型规模和信号强度的假设未获确认。

Conclusion: Ignition Index提供了首个经过验证的定量桥梁，连接GWT的动力学预测与机制可解释性，具有9.6倍的测量选择性和架构级别的判别能力，这在以往的扩展性文献中未被表征。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [149] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Woodpecker Distillation（啄木鸟蒸馏）框架，通过对比局部干预（成功与失败的弱模型补丁）来构建纠正性的教师分布，并将其蒸馏到强模型中，以修复大语言模型在推理任务中的局部推理错误，实验表明该方法优于直接的模仿蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理任务中经常失败，但这些失败通常不是全局能力的不足，而是中间步骤中局部推理错误导致的。这些错误常常可以通过插入简短的补丁来修复。然而，直接对弱模型的补丁或修复轨迹进行微调无法可靠地将这种纠正效果内化到强模型中，说明有效的信号不在于干预文本本身，而在于它如何重塑模型的未来推理分布。

Method: 提出Woodpecker Distillation框架：1) 在相同的推理前缀处对比成功与不成功的弱模型补丁；2) 利用这些对比干预诱导的未来token预测来构建纠正性的教师分布；3) 将此信号蒸馏到强模型中。核心思想是从对比性局部干预中学习，而非直接模仿干预文本。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，Woodpecker Distillation持续提升强模型的性能，并且优于直接模仿的基线方法。

Conclusion: 局部推理错误是可修复的，而有效的训练信号来源于干预如何重塑模型的未来推理分布而非干预文本本身；通过对比局部干预进行蒸馏可以更有效地利用弱模型的纠正能力来提升强模型。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [150] [Integrating Implicit and Explicit Relational Biases through Graph-Based Multiple Instance Learning: A Case Study in Skin Lesion Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06037)
*Rafał Buler,Jakub Buler,Maciej Bobowicz,Michał Grochowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种双层关系学习框架用于图像分类，结合隐式关系建模（卷积掩码自编码器学习patch间关系）和显式关系建模（图注意力网络在不同图拓扑上进行消息传递），在ISIC-2018和ISIC-2019皮肤病变诊断基准上取得了优于基线EfficientNetB3的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系归纳偏置对于捕捉数据间的结构依赖至关重要，但现有方法往往只关注隐式表示学习或显式结构建模的单一层面，缺乏两者结合的系统研究。

Method: 先以EfficientNetB3作为基线，然后采用patch策略，利用卷积掩码自编码器通过自监督重建学习隐式patch间关系；在此基础上引入显式关系建模，将学习到的嵌入组织成网格、随机和k近邻等不同图拓扑，并通过图注意力网络进行消息传递。

Result: 在ISIC-2018上，基线平衡准确率为76.17%，隐式patch关系建模提升至77.12%，网格结构的图注意力网络进一步达到79.27%；在ISIC-2019上，隐式方法达到59.84%，隐式与显式结合达到60.67%。

Conclusion: 隐式patch间建模与显式图消息传递的结合能有效提升图像分类性能，网格结构的图注意力网络表现最佳，验证了双层关系学习框架的有效性。

Abstract: Relational inductive biases are essential for capturing structural dependencies among data. This study investigates a dual-level relational framework for image classification, bridging the gap between implicit representation learning and explicit structural modelling. We begin by establishing a baseline using an EfficientNetB3 architecture. To move beyond standard convolutional biases, we adopt a patch-based strategy, employing a convolutional masked autoencoder to learn implicit inter-patch relationships through self-supervised reconstruction. We then extend this approach by incorporating explicit relational modelling, organizing the learned embeddings into various graph topologies, including grid-based, random, and k-nearest neighbour structures. Experimental results on the ISIC-2018 and ISIC-2019 skin lesion diagnosis benchmarks show that combining implicit inter-patch modelling with explicit graph-based message passing yields the best performance. On the ISIC-2018 test set, the baseline model achieves a balanced accuracy of 76.17%, which improves to 77.12% with implicit patch-based relational modelling. The fully integrated grid-structured Graph Attention Network further increases performance to 79.27%. Similarly, on ISIC-2019, the implicit approach reaches 59.84% balanced accuracy, while the combination of implicit and explicit modelling yields 60.67%.

</details>


### [151] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测模型中，用基于临床指南的类别化编码替代连续型预测变量是否会影响模型性能。结果显示，在三个治疗队列中的两个队列，完全类别化模型与连续变量模型在统计上无显著差异，且全局特征重要性排序保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测中已取得较高准确率，但其解释方式与临床医生的推理逻辑不一致，限制了临床应用。临床医生用户研究呼吁采用与指南一致的截断值，因此作者希望探究能否在不牺牲性能的前提下，用临床信息化的类别编码替代连续型预测变量。

Method: 基于多中心欧洲注册登记数据，将患者按三种治疗方案分层为三个队列。分别训练标准梯度提升模型与完全类别化的梯度提升模型，后者采用卒中指南对齐的、治疗特异性的阈值对连续预测变量进行离散化。通过对比两组模型的预测性能与全局特征重要性进行评价。

Result: 在三个治疗队列中的两个队列，完全类别化模型与连续变量标准模型的预测性能在统计上无显著差异；在一个队列中出现显著的预测准确率下降。全局特征重要性排序在所有治疗组中保持一致，表明将连续预测变量离散化为指南类别能够保留预后因素的核心层级结构。

Conclusion: 基于指南的类别化编码是卒中结局预测模型中可行的设计选择，它可以提升模型的可解释性与临床对齐程度，同时仅在部分治疗队列中带来轻微的预测性能代价。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [152] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILLTRACE是一个用于LLM代理技能复用审计的多轨迹溯源框架，通过提取表达、实现和操作三条轨迹，在审计时确定性比较缓存轨迹，并在SKILLTRACE-BENCH基准（820个正例、751个负例）上达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898，同时通过36,446个技能的野审计证明其能发现可操作的复用审查队列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理生态系统围绕可复用技能快速增长，技能成为市场制品后，其复用审计不再等同于普通代码克隆检测。现有检测器只针对单模态源代码或整体包相似度，但技能复用证据分散在文本、实现代码片段和操作结构中，导致它们可能遗漏仅保留技能某一部分的复用情况。

Method: SKILLTRACE提取三条溯源轨迹：表达轨迹（Expression）、实现轨迹（Implementation）和操作轨迹（Operational）。操作轨迹以技能操作图（SOG）表示，捕获激活、流程和资源流动结构。LLM仅在摄入时辅助一次操作轨迹提取，审计时确定性比较缓存轨迹，并针对同功能严格负例校准每条轨迹，最后报告支持复用决策的轨迹证据。

Result: 在SKILLTRACE-BENCH基准上（包含820个转换后的复用正例，覆盖100个市场锚点，以及751个负对照），SKILLTRACE达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898。另外通过36,446个技能的野审计证明，轨迹归因证据能比仓库级基线浮现出更可操作的复用审查队列。

Conclusion: SKILLTRACE通过多轨迹溯源方法有效解决了LLM代理技能复用的审计难题，能够在技能市场背景下更准确地识别部分复用，并提供可归因的审计证据，超越了传统代码克隆检测方案的局限。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [153] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR），一种关系级因果抽象范式，将具体因果对转化为泛化的抽象因果逻辑，并构建了多智能体CACI系统和AR-GCAE编码器，在事件预测任务中显著提升了低频率长尾和未见事件组合上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向事件中心的智能分析系统（如风险预警、决策支持、叙事理解）高度依赖显式因果事件知识。然而，现有实例级因果对在低频长尾和未见事件组合上存在严重的泛化缺陷，限制了系统的实际应用效果。

Method: (1) 提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR）范式，将具体因果对转化为保留内在因果关系的泛化抽象因果逻辑；(2) 设计多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳（CACI）系统，结合相似性约束聚类，从噪声原始因果数据中提取可信的AECR；(3) 构建两个完整的AECR知识库；(4) 提出抽象规则引导的因果注意力编码器（AR-GCAE），通过规则引导注意力层和门控表示融合将检索到的AECR注入因果图事件预测（CGEP）基准任务。

Result: 定量实验结果表明，应用AECR显著增强了事件因果推理的泛化能力，并为事件预测带来一致的性能提升，其中在稀有和未见事件样本上的增益最为突出。

Conclusion: AECR作为一种关系级因果抽象范式，能够有效弥补实例级因果知识的泛化不足，为事件因果推理系统提供可跨场景迁移的抽象因果规则，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>


### [154] [Otter: A Time-Aware, History-Conditioned Human Chess AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05206)
*Tarun Kumar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Otter是一个仅15.3M参数的人类棋手走子预测AI，通过将下棋建模为时间感知的序列过程（而非孤立处理每个局面），结合最近20步的走子历史编码器和时间控制模块，在6.1亿个局面、1.17亿局Lichess快棋对局上训练，仅用单卡T4 GPU训练30天，达到55.23% top-1和90.95% top-5的走子预测准确率，超越前SOTA模型Maia 2，且参数更少、训练数据更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人类棋手走子预测模型（如Maia 2）通常将每个局面视为独立位置进行处理，忽略了棋局过程中的时间动态性和走子序列的上下文信息。作者认为人类下棋是一个随时间演化的顺序过程，需要同时考虑走子历史（包括开局偏好、位置漂移和局内行为倾向）以及计时压力对决策的影响，因此提出将棋局建模为时间感知的顺序活动。

Method: Otter采用两个条件信号：一是移动历史编码器，基于最近20步走子序列来条件化预测，捕捉开局偏好、局面漂移和局内行为倾向；二是时间控制模块，根据时钟压力调节预测输出。模型在1.17亿局Lichess快棋对局（共61亿个局面）上训练，总计仅需30天时间在单张T4 GPU上完成。

Result: Otter在top-1预测准确率达到55.23%、top-5达到90.95%，超越此前SOTA的人类棋手模型Maia 2，同时使用更少的参数和训练数据。在11个Elo区间（<1100到>=2000）的评估中，最高准确率为57.38%，出现在1900-1999区间。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，将国际象棋建模为时间感知的序列活动而非仅基于位置的静态问题，可以在使用更小模型的情况下获得更高的人类走子预测准确率，验证了序列上下文和时间压力在人类行为建模中的重要性。

Abstract: Otter is a 15.3M-parameter human chess AI that predicts human move selection by modeling play as a time-aware, sequential process rather than treating each position in isolation. It combines two conditioning signals: (1) a move history encoder that conditions predictions on the last 20 moves, capturing opening preferences, positional drift, and intra-game behavioral tendencies; and (2) a time control module that modulates predictions based on clock pressure. Otter is trained on 6.1 billion positions from 117 million Lichess rapid games over 30 days on a single T4 GPU.
  Otter achieves 55.23% top-1 and 90.95% top-5 move-prediction accuracy, surpassing the prior state-of-the-art human chess model, Maia 2, with far fewer parameters and less training data. Across 11 Elo brackets (<1100 to >=2000), accuracy peaks at 57.38% in the 1900-1999 bracket. These results show that modeling chess as a time-aware, sequential activity yields more human-accurate move prediction than position-only approaches, using a smaller model. Code, trained models, and complete training logs are publicly released.

</details>


### [155] [SearchAuditor: Auditing and Attributing Failures in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05212)
*Zhixiang Liang,Yifei Liu,Yidan Huang,Haozhe Zhao,Beichen Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan,Qiong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SearchAuditBench基准，用于评估LLM审计员定位、归因和修复深度搜索代理失败的能力，并提出了SearchAuditor多视角审计框架，显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索代理在长时间交互中容易因小错误传播而导致错误答案，且诊断失败需要人工检查极长的执行轨迹，超出人类能力范围，亟需自动化审计工具来减轻人工负担。

Method: 构建包含1,243条失败轨迹的SearchAuditBench基准（平均73.1条消息、65.1K token），来自8个开源模型在5个深度搜索基准上的结果，每条轨迹都有专家标注的关键错误步骤、搜索特定的根本原因和带评分标准的参考修复方案。提出SearchAuditor多视角审计框架，通过基于证据的裁决来定位、归因和修复搜索代理失败。

Result: 最强基线（GPT-5.5）端到端通过率仅26.6%；SearchAuditor在多个前沿模型上始终优于所有基线，实现32.3%的端到端通过率，且其修复能使代理从错误中更好地恢复。

Conclusion: SearchAuditor通过多视角证据裁决的审计框架能有效提升搜索代理失败诊断与修复能力，减少对人工审计的依赖。

Abstract: Deep search agents tackle challenging questions through long-horizon web interactions, a process that is both complex and fragile: small reasoning errors may propagate through long, noisy trajectories into fluent but incorrect answers. Diagnosing such failures is difficult, requiring the manual inspection of extremely long execution traces, which could be beyond human capacity. We therefore introduce SearchAuditBench, a benchmark that evaluates whether LLM auditors can localize, attribute, and repair these failures, thereby reducing the human burden. SearchAuditBench comprises 1,243 failed trajectories, averaging 73.1 messages and 65.1K tokens, collected from eight open-weight models on five deep-search benchmarks, each expert-annotated with the critical error step, a search-specific root cause, and a reference repair with grading rubrics. We further propose SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective auditing framework that effectively localizes, attributes, and repairs search-agent failures through evidence-grounded adjudication. Experimental results show that even the strongest baseline, when powered by a frontier model like GPT-5.5, attains only a 26.6% end-to-end pass rate. In contrast, our SearchAuditor consistently outperforms all baselines across different frontier models, achieving an end-to-end pass rate of 32.3%, and resuming failed runs with its repairs enables agents to better recover from errors.

</details>


### [156] [PD-GS: Phoneme-Driven 3DGS for Audio-Driven Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05218)
*Ao Fu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PD-GS通过在3D高斯溅射说话人模型中引入时间对齐的音素令牌和语言学融合模块(LFM)，利用可学习的门控机制自适应融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，解决了说话人渲染中唇部运动过度平滑和"漏嘴"伪影问题，在HDTF数据集上取得了最佳的唇部几何精度(LMD 2.66)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS实现快速逼真的说话人头像渲染，但唇部发音精度不足：唇部运动常过度平滑，且可能违反双唇闭合等硬性发音约束，产生"漏嘴"伪影。其根本原因是离散、短暂的发音事件在回归目标下从连续声学嵌入中推断，导致预测偏向平均化的嘴型。现有自监督语音编码器虽提供丰富的韵律和语音线索，但缺乏明确的、帧对齐的语言学目标来可靠区分闭合级事件。

Method: PD-GS通过ASR和强制对齐流水线获得时间对齐的音素令牌，增强3DGS说话人模型。核心组件为Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)，通过可学习的门控机制自适应融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，在发音关键片段强化音素引导的同时保留平滑的音频驱动动态。模型仅使用单目视频训练，通过图像重建和唇部地标监督优化。

Result: 在HDTF数据集上，PD-GS在比较基线中取得最佳唇部几何性能(LMD 2.66)，并在具有挑战性的音素序列中定性减少了闭合违规，生成更符合语言学的神经化身。

Conclusion: 引入显式时间对齐的音素令牌并有选择性地融合到音频驱动的3DGS管线中，是解决说话人头像唇部发音不准确问题的有效途径，能够实现既平滑又符合硬性发音约束的唇部渲染。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, photorealistic talking-head rendering, yet accurate lip articulation remains elusive: mouth motion is often over-smoothed and may violate hard articulatory constraints such as bilabial closures, producing the notorious ``leaky mouth'' artifact. A key difficulty is that brief, discrete articulatory events are inferred from a continuous acoustic embedding under a regression objective, which biases predictions toward averaged mouth configurations. While modern self-supervised speech encoders provide rich prosodic and phonetic cues, they do not provide an explicit, frame-aligned linguistic target that reliably disambiguates closure-level events. We propose \textbf{Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)}, which augments a 3DGS talker with time-aligned phoneme tokens obtained from an automatic ASR and forced-alignment pipeline. Our core component, the \textbf{Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)}, adaptively fuses continuous audio context with discrete phoneme embeddings through a learned gate, allowing the model to preserve smooth audio-driven dynamics while strengthening phoneme guidance on articulation-critical segments. PD-GS is trained purely from monocular video using image reconstruction and lip landmark supervision. On HDTF, PD-GS achieves the best lip geometry among the compared baselines (LMD 2.66) and qualitatively reduces closure violations in challenging phoneme sequences, yielding more linguistically faithful neural avatars.

</details>


### [157] [When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219)
*Junzhuo Liu,Weiwei Li,Jun Ling,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SMRC-SD方法，在多轮交互式智能体的特权蒸馏学习中，通过状态匹配路由和情境化自蒸馏，解决学生轨迹与参考轨迹状态不匹配的问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特权蒸馏为多轮智能体提供密集监督，但学生动作会改变执行状态，导致其访问的状态可能与参考轨迹覆盖的状态不匹配，从而产生状态-参考不匹配问题，使参考成为不可靠的指导来源。

Method: SMRC-SD包含两个核心组件：(1) State-Matched Routing——在每个回合验证学生当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹中的某个支持状态匹配，仅对匹配的状态应用蒸馏；(2) Contextualized Self-Distillation——对每个匹配状态，从成功轨迹构建状态条件化的教师上下文，将监督锚定在实际到达的状态上。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，SMRC-SD一致优于无条件成功全路径蒸馏。使用Qwen3-1.7B时，ALFWorld任务成功率从0.746提升至0.865，WebShop从0.574提升至0.693。路由和上下文消融实验支持两类贡献：选择局部可支持的回合和构建状态兼容的教师上下文。

Conclusion: 选择性应用特权蒸馏（仅状态匹配时）并构建状态条件化教师上下文，能有效解决状态-参考不匹配问题，是多轮交互式智能体更可靠的蒸馏策略。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multi-turn agents by allowing a synchronized teacher to re-score the student's response at every turn with access to training-only references, such as successful trajectories. In interactive environments, however, the student's preceding actions continually change the execution state. As the student takes different actions or completes subgoals in a different order, its rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, making the reference an unreliable source of guidance for the state actually reached. Applying privileged distillation indiscriminately therefore creates state--reference mismatch. This mismatch motivates a central objective: providing privileged reference guidance that remains compatible with the student's current execution state. We introduce State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation (SMRC-SD), which explicitly determines when and how a privileged trajectory should guide an on-policy student. At each turn, SMRC-SD verifies whether the student's current execution state matches a supported state along the reference trajectory. Distillation is applied only at matched states, filtering out turns for which the reference lacks locally compatible guidance. For each matched state, SMRC-SD further constructs state-conditioned teacher context from the successful trajectory, grounding supervision in the state actually reached. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD consistently outperforms unconditional successful full-path distillation. With Qwen3-1.7B, it improves task success from $0.746$ to $0.865$ on ALFWorld and from $0.574$ to $0.693$ on WebShop. Controlled routing and context ablations support both selecting locally supported turns and constructing state-compatible teacher context as contributors to these gains. Code is available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD.

</details>


### [158] [Small Foundation Models of Human Cognition and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05224)
*Nick Oh,Fernand Gobet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型在心理行为数据上微调后的认知代理能力，训练了从1.35亿到140亿参数的14个模型（四个架构族）在Psych-101数据集（1070万次试验级选择，来自160个实验）上。发现分布内性能对规模不敏感（0.6B-1B参数即可匹配70B基线），但分布外泛化呈现明显更陡峭的规模梯度。通过剥离四种提示通道和置换试验顺序的消融实验，证明模型依赖刺激和反馈内容而非仅凭选择历史，且对试验顺序的敏感性取决于任务结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑基于人类行为数据微调的大语言模型作为通用认知代理的有效性——模型规模是否必要、模型是真正处理任务结构还是利用统计捷径（如仅依赖选择历史）仍是未解问题。

Method: 在Psych-101数据集上训练14个不同规模（135M-14B）和四个架构族的模型；在分布内和分布外条件下比较缩放规律；通过逐步剥离任务指令、实验刺激、结果反馈和选择历史四种提示通道，以及置换试验顺序的诊断实验，揭示模型使用的信息类型。

Result: 分布内性能呈现天花板效应，规模影响甚微，0.6B-1B参数即足以匹配70B基线；分布外泛化则呈现明显规模优势。剥离刺激和反馈内容导致75.7%的学习信息被破坏并使模型低于随机水平，证明模型并非依赖选择历史。试验顺序置换实验显示模型对独立试验任务不敏感，但对依赖先前响应决定顺序的任务敏感。

Conclusion: 小型认知微调模型可作为心理实验的噪声天花板估计器，但其适用范围受限于训练中见过的范式。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned on human behavioural data have emerged as general-purpose cognitive proxies, but the scale this requires, and whether these models process task structure or exploit statistical shortcuts, remain open questions. We train fourteen models from 135M to 14B parameters across four architecture families on Psych-101, a dataset of 10.7 million trial-level choices from 160 experiments. In-distribution, scale barely matters. The models fall within a narrow band, as though against a ceiling, and 0.6B to 1B parameters suffice to match a 70B baseline on held-out participants. Out-of-distribution, that band opens into a markedly steeper scaling gradient, with larger models clearly advantaged in generalisation to novel task structure. To determine what information these models use, we run two diagnostics. We progressively strip four prompt channels -- task instructions, experimental stimuli, outcome feedback, and choice history -- across 27 experiments, and permute trial order. Masking the content of stimuli and feedback destroys 75.7% of learned information and pushes models below chance, demonstrating that choice history alone does not account for performance. Permutation reveals invariance on tasks with independent trials but sensitivity where trial order is determined by prior responses. Small cognitively fine-tuned models therefore show promise as noise ceiling estimators for psychological experiments, though their scope remains bounded by the paradigms seen in training.

</details>


### [159] [Project2Task: Graph-Guided Project-Level Planning for Autonomous Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05225)
*Huirui Xu,Runtao Xu,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Project2Task，一个图引导的项目级规划层，将研究项目brief分解为有界任务契约，通过创新原子有向谱系图和伯努利块模型实现水平/垂直/混合组合分解，生成依赖感知的任务契约。在10个项目brief（约30个任务）上，平均质量得分7.15（对比Brief基线4.58），并与AutoResearchClaw集成将下游任务准确率从0.536提升至0.759。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究代理可以执行文献检索、假设提出、代码生成和实验运行等任务，但研究项目并非简单的较大任务，而是一个需要推进的长时程议程，包含多个有界任务、平行替代方案和依赖感知的序列。现有单任务系统要么将项目视为一个超大任务，要么产生扁平模糊的任务集合，或需要人工协调任务边界与执行顺序，缺乏自动的项目到任务的规划能力。

Method: Project2Task包含三个核心组件：(1) 将项目brief中的候选贡献表示为"创新原子"（innovation atoms），并组织在有向谱系图中；(2) 使用轻量级伯努利块模型目标函数，在水平、垂直和混合组合分解之间进行选择；(3) 生成有界任务契约，包含明确的贡献归属、目标、输入、预期产物、评估要求、边界约束、依赖关系和执行顺序，并修复任务重叠和缺失的执行字段。任务契约与下游研究执行器无关。

Result: 在包含10个项目brief、约30个任务的基准上，基于手稿的消融评估顯示Project2Task的平均质量得分为7.15，优于Brief Baseline的4.58和Topic-only Setting的5.31。将Project2Task契约与AutoResearchClaw集成后，平均下游任务准确率从0.536提升至0.759。

Conclusion: 显式的项目到任务规划对于生成连贯、非冗余且可执行的研究任务组合具有明显价值。Project2Task通过将项目级意图转化为结构化的、依赖感知的任务契约，有效弥合了高层研究目标与低层任务执行之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Research agents can increasingly search literature, propose hypotheses, generate code, run experiments, and draft manuscripts from a single topic. However, a research project is not merely a larger task: it is a long-horizon agenda that must be advanced through multiple bounded tasks with distinct but related objectives, parallel alternatives, and dependency-aware sequences. Existing single-task systems often treat the project as one oversized task, produce a flat set of vague or overlapping tasks, or leave task boundaries and execution order to manual coordination. We introduce Project2Task, a graph-guided project-level planning layer for autonomous research. Given a project brief, it represents candidate contributions as innovation atoms and organizes them in a directed lineage graph. A lightweight Bernoulli block-model objective selects among horizontal, vertical, and hybrid portfolio decompositions. Project2Task then generates bounded tasks with explicit contribution ownership, repairs overlaps and missing execution fields, and emits dependency-aware task contracts that specify objectives, inputs, expected artifacts, evaluation requirements, boundary constraints, dependencies, and execution order. The contracts are independent of any particular downstream research executor and support integration of task outputs into a coherent project-level result. On a benchmark of ten project briefs yielding roughly 30 tasks, manuscript-based portfolio evaluation gives Project2Task an average quality score of 7.15, compared with 4.58 for the Brief Baseline and 5.31 for the Topic-only Setting. Integrating its contracts with AutoResearchClaw increases average downstream task accuracy from 0.536 to 0.759. These results demonstrate the value of explicit project-to-task planning for producing coherent, non-redundant, and executable research-task portfolios.

</details>


### [160] [TriQua: Reconciling Granularity and Context in Factuality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05228)
*Jin Liu,Steffen Thoma,Achim Rettinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TriQua框架，通过根据事实复杂度灵活建模（简单声明用标准三元组，复杂声明用带辅助限定符的超关系事实），解决了LLM事实性评估中"分解-验证"范式的粒度与上下文保真之间的矛盾。TriQuaScore用于量化结构化事实单元的事实性，实验表明其在事实性评分与证据验证方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的"分解-验证"范式在LLM事实性评估中面临根本性权衡：原子事实（一句话只传达一个信息单元）往往丢失关键上下文，而更宽泛的语句又缺乏精确评估所需的粒度。需要一种能够根据事实复杂度自适应建模的方法来解决这一矛盾。

Method: TriQua框架包含两个核心部分：（1）自适应事实建模——简单声明提取为标准三元组，复杂声明表示为带辅助上下文限定符的超关系事实，在保持原子性的同时保留必要上下文；（2）验证过程——直接在特定三元组和限定符上标注具体错误，提供细粒度的可解释错误检测。同时提出TriQuaScore用于量化这些结构化事实单元的事实性。

Result: 实证评估表明：（1）TriQuaScore与人工标注的事实性评分高度一致；（2）TriQua实现了稳健的分解质量；（3）在基于证据的事实验证任务中，TriQua优于现有的基于分解的框架。

Conclusion: TriQua通过灵活建模事实复杂度，在原子性与上下文保真之间取得平衡，同时为错误检测提供细粒度的可解释性，是一种更优的LLM事实性评估方案。

Abstract: The "decompose-then-verify" paradigm for LLM factuality evaluation faces a fundamental trade-off: atomic facts, i.e., one sentence conveying one unit of information, often omit essential context, while broader statements lack the granularity needed for precise assessment. To address this, we introduce TriQua, a framework that flexibly models facts based on their complexity. Simple claims are extracted as standard triples, while complex claims are represented as hyperrelational facts by attaching auxiliary contextual qualifiers. This adaptive structure preserves the necessary context for accurate retrieval and verification without sacrificing atomicity. Furthermore, TriQua's verification process directly annotates concrete errors within specific triples and qualifiers, providing fine-grained explainability for error detection. Alongside the framework, we propose TriQuaScore to quantify the factuality of these structured fact units. Empirical evaluations show that TriQuaScore strongly aligns with human annotated factuality scores, TriQua achieves robust decomposition quality, and outperforms existing decomposition-based frameworks in evidence-based fact verification.

</details>


### [161] [Coherence-Oriented Dream Scene Visualisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05233)
*Azra Açıl,Simon Colton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了"梦境场景可视化系统"(DSV)，该系统可将书面梦境描述转化为按时间顺序排列的四格图像序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梦境通常情感强烈但难以直接传达，作者希望设计一个系统将文字梦境描述转化为可视化的图像序列，帮助人们更直观地理解和分享梦境。

Method: DSV系统包含两个核心组件：首先利用大语言模型将梦境描述拆分为四个按时间顺序排列的部分；然后使用文本到图像模型为每个部分生成图像，并通过迭代再生机制确保视觉连贯性，若图像与文本不匹配则重新生成。

Result: 在DreamBank的50个梦境可视化样本上进行了评估，通过使用CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL视觉语言模型的客观指标报告了质量、保真度和连贯性的结果。

Conclusion: DSV实现了从梦境文字描述到多图序列可视化的自动转换，并通过客观评估验证了其在图像质量、内容保真度和序列连贯性方面的表现。

Abstract: Dreams can be emotionally intense but difficult to communicate. We describe the Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV) system which turns written dream descriptions into a temporal sequence of four panel images visualising the dream. This starts with a large language model prompted to split a dream description into four chronological parts. Then a text-to-image model produces images for each part with visual coherence maintained across the sequence, and DSV regenerates any image not suitably matching the text. We evaluate DSV over 50 visualisations from dream descriptions in DreamBank, and report quality, fidelity and coherence results via objective measures employing the CLIP, DINOv2 and Qwen2-VL vision-language models.

</details>


### [162] [Search2Skill: Skill Distillation Beyond Knowledge Boundaries Via Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05245)
*Muyang Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Yongshi Ye,Wuyunsiqin,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Ye Wang,Jinyang Zhang,Longyue Wang,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Search2Skill框架，通过自动识别LLM代理的能力缺口、搜索外部资源并将检索证据蒸馏为结构化可复用技能，克服现有自进化技能方法受限于模型已有知识的局限，在八个专家领域基准上超越搜索增强和轨迹式技能学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自进化技能方法仅从模型参数知识或轨迹中内部构建技能，受限于模型已知内容。然而专业技能的领域惯例和标准流程往往超出模型知识边界，难以仅靠智能体自身引出。因此需要一种能够自动搜索外部来源来弥补能力缺口的方法。

Method: 提出Search2Skill框架，包含三个核心环节：(1) 自动识别智能体的能力缺口；(2) 搜索外部来源以弥补这些缺口；(3) 将检索到的证据蒸馏为结构化、可复用的技能。该框架通过基于rubric的强化学习方案进行联合优化，同时改进何时搜索、如何搜索以及如何生成技能。

Result: 在来自三个基准的八个专家级领域上，Search2Skill在流式和保留评估协议下均一致优于搜索增强和基于轨迹的技能学习基线。进一步分析表明，性能提升来源于技能抽象而非原始检索证据，且所获技能可跨模型规模迁移。

Conclusion: Search2Skill通过外部搜索与强化学习优化的结合，有效突破了模型自身知识边界，为LLM代理在专家领域的自我进化提供了一种可扩展且可迁移的范式。

Abstract: Reusable skills, which encapsulate the procedural knowledge required to solve real-world professional tasks, offer LLM-based agents a path toward self-evolution in expert domains. Existing self-evolving skill methods construct skills internally from the model's parametric knowledge or trajectories, and are therefore bounded by what the model already knows. However, the domain conventions and standard procedures underlying professional skills often lie beyond this boundary and are hard to elicit from the agent alone. To address this issue, we therefore propose a novel framework, Search2Skill, that automatically identifies the agent's capability gaps, searches external sources to address them, and distills the retrieved evidence into structured, reusable skills. Specifically, Search2Skill is optimized by a rubric-based reinforcement learning scheme that jointly improves when to search, how to search, and how to generate skills. Experiments on eight expert-level domains from three benchmarks show that Search2Skill consistently outperforms both search-augmented and trajectory-based skill-learning baselines under both streaming and held-out evaluation protocols. Further analyses show that the gains arise from skill abstraction rather than raw retrieved evidence, and that the acquired skills transfer across model scales.

</details>


### [163] [LUNAR: Benchmarking Personalized Large Language Models on UNiversal User BehAvioR Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05246)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yongzhi Tong,Zelin Fu,Pengde Zhao,Yanmei Jiang,Jiang Feng,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUNAR是首个用于评估LLM在纵向应用交互历史上跨通用日常生活领域（衣、食、住、行）进行跨域行为个性化响应的基准，基于多阶段粗到细合成管线构建，实验中揭示行为日志访问是必要但不充分的，证据选择和跨域整合才是关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化LLM基准主要依赖文本人物角色或孤立的行为信号，缺乏对跨域行为个性化（即响应需基于异构日常活动）的评估能力，无法真实反映LLM在多样化生活场景中的个性化水平。

Method: 提出LUNAR基准，采用多阶段粗到细的合成管线，基于真实行为模式构建纵向应用交互历史数据集，覆盖衣、食、住、行等通用日常生活域，并通过保真度分析验证合成数据与真实行为分布的一致性。

Result: 在19个主流LLM上的实验表明：行为日志访问是必要但非充分的；更多上下文或更大模型并不能保证更好的个性化表现；有效个性化取决于跨域相关证据的选择与整合；直接检索细粒度行为记录优于压缩记忆；更强的个性化可能以隐私保护为代价。

Conclusion: 指明证据选择、跨域整合和隐私控制是个性化LLM面临的关键挑战，为后续研究提供了明确的改进方向。

Abstract: Existing personalized LLM benchmarks primarily rely on textual personas or isolated behavioral signals, providing limited evaluation of cross-domain behavioral personalization, where responses must be grounded in heterogeneous daily-life activities. To address this gap, we introduce LUNAR, the first benchmark for evaluating how LLMs personalize responses from longitudinal app interaction histories across universal daily-life domains, including clothing, food, housing, and mobility. To support scalable benchmark construction while mitigating data sparsity and privacy concerns, LUNAR uses a multi-stage coarse-to-fine synthesis pipeline grounded in real-world behavioral patterns. Fidelity analyses show closer alignment with real behavioral distributions than other synthetic benchmarks. Experiments on 19 mainstream LLMs show that access to behavioral logs is necessary but not sufficient for deep personalization: neither more context nor larger models guarantees better performance; effective personalization depends on selecting and integrating relevant evidence across domains. Direct retrieval of fine-grained behavioral records consistently outperforms compressed memory, while stronger personalization can come at the cost of privacy protection. These findings identify evidence selection, cross-domain integration, and privacy control as key challenges for personalized LLMs.

</details>


### [164] [WorldClaw: Agentic 3D Open-World Generation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05248)
*Chunchao Guo,Jinpeng Li,Yang Li,Zilong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldClaw是一个完全智能体驱动的、从粗到细的开放世界3D场景生成框架，能够从开放文本提示中生成大规模、可自由探索的3D世界，同时保持全局空间一致性、丰富局部内容和可编辑的显式资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从开放式文本生成大规模、可自由探索的3D世界具有挑战性，因为系统需要同时维护全局空间连贯性、丰富的局部内容以及可供下游编辑和重用显式资产，现有方法难以在多个维度上同时满足这些要求。

Method: WorldClaw采用完全智能体化（agentic）的从粗到细（coarse-to-fine）框架：规划智能体将文本提示转化为包含区域、地形、资产、材质和空间关系的结构化规格；然后构建基于语义布局的全局连贯地形基础，包括可复用资产、生成或程序化材质以及区域感知高度场；对于细节要求高的区域，生成地形条件化组合，重建可编辑的纹理网格并恢复其在地形上的摆放位置；渲染基智能体进一步细化地形、物体、外观和接触关系。

Result: 在多样的开放世界提示上，WorldClaw能够生成具有连贯空间组织、视觉上引人入胜的局部内容以及可编辑的实例级资产的大规模场景，同时保持一致的全局地形结构。

Conclusion: WorldClaw证明了基于多智能体协作的从粗到细框架能够有效解决开放世界3D场景生成中全局一致性与局部丰富性、可编辑性之间的平衡问题，为大规模可探索3D世界生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generating large-scale, freely explorable 3D worlds from open-ended text remains challenging because a system must jointly maintain global spatial coherence, rich local content, and explicit assets suitable for downstream editing and reuse. We present WorldClaw, a fully agentic, coarse-to-fine framework for open-world 3D scene generation. Planning agents translate a text prompt into a structured specification of regions, terrain, assets, materials, and spatial relations. WorldClaw then builds a globally coherent terrain foundation from semantic layouts, reusable assets, generative or procedural materials, and a region-aware height field. For detail-demanding regions, it generates terrain-conditioned compositions, reconstructs editable textured meshes, and recovers their placement on the terrain; render-based agents further refine terrain, objects, appearance, and contacts. Across diverse open-world prompts, WorldClaw produces large-scale scenes with coherent spatial organization, visually compelling local content, and editable instance-level assets while preserving a consistent global terrain structure.

</details>


### [165] [Posture and Sustainment Optimization Under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05256)
*Amelie Norris,Alyssa Lee,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CEV优化器和RobustCEV扩展，用于解决军事预承诺姿态（PSA）问题，即如何在冲突场景解决前将军事资产分配到战区位置。CEV通过最大化场景加权期望姿态效率来布置资产，RobustCEV则迭代对抗根据观察更新目标分布的贝叶斯对手。在印太基地环境（20个资产、5个战区位置）的三个实验中，贪心基线因地理覆盖不足产生25.1%的永久效率损失和57.3%的应对能力崩溃；CEV在威胁分布携带地理信号时比贪心恢复最高19.8%的效率；RobustCEV在面对采用欺骗性威胁先验的自适应对手时，比朴素优化器恢复最高158%的效率。所有结果经配对t检验（Bonferroni校正）和两层方差分解验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预承诺姿态（pre-commitment posture）——在冲突场景未解决前将军事资产分配到战区位置——是联合作战规划中一个关键但形式上未解决的问题。当前实践依赖贪心启发式算法，仅最大化价值而忽略地理覆盖，且结构上易受瞄准高战略价值位置的对手攻击。因此需要一种对抗鲁棒的姿态优化方法。

Method: 将PSA问题建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），覆盖资产、战区位置和时间步。提出两种方法：(1) CEV（Composite Expected Value）优化器：通过最大化场景加权期望姿态效率来放置资产，利用威胁场景分布；(2) RobustCEV扩展：迭代对抗贝叶斯对手，对手根据观察到的资产布置更新其目标分布，从而实现对抗鲁棒性。

Result: 在印太基地环境（20个资产、5个战区位置）的三个实验中：(1) 贪心基线产生25.1%的永久姿态效率损失（因地理覆盖不足）和57.3%的场景加权应对能力崩溃（面对价值相关对抗威胁时）；(2) CEV优化器在威胁分布含地理信号时比贪心恢复最高19.8%效率，5-20个精选场景足以捕获大部分增益；(3) RobustCEV在面对采用欺骗性威胁先验的自适应对手时，比朴素优化器恢复最高158%效率。结果经配对t检验（Bonferroni校正）和两层方差分解验证为结构性属性而非采样伪影。

Conclusion: 研究表明，系统性的场景加权对抗鲁棒姿态优化能显著超越传统的贪心启发式方法，性能差距是结构性的。CEV和RobustCEV框架为后勤规划中的姿态分配提供了经过验证的优化路径，特别在对手采用自适应或欺骗性策略时优势更为显著。

Abstract: Pre-commitment posture, the assignment of military assets to theater locations before conflict scenarios resolve, is a critical and formally unsolved problem in joint operational planning. Current practice relies on greedy heuristics that maximize value and ignore geographic coverage and are structurally vulnerable to adversaries that target high-strategic value locations. This paper presents a scenario-weighted adversarially robust posture optimization engine for the Posture and sustainability allocation (PSA) problem, modeled as a finite-horizon Markov Decision Process over assets, theater locations, and time steps. We introduce the Composite Expected Value (CEV) optimizer, which places assets by maximizing scenario-weighted expected posture efficiency over a distribution of threat scenarios, and the RobustCEV extension, which iterates against a Bayesian adversary that updates its targeting distribution in response to observed placement. Across three experiments in an Indo-Pacific basing environment with 20 assets and 5 theater locations, we demonstrate that: (1) the greedy baseline incurs a permanent 25.1% posture efficiency penalty due to geographic under-coverage and a 57.3% scenario-weighted readiness collapse under value-correlated adversarial threat; (2) the CEV optimizer recovers up to 19.8% efficiency over greedy when the threat distribution carries a geographic signal, with a curated set of 5 to 20 scenarios sufficient to capture the majority of this gain; and (3) the RobustCEV extension recovers up to 158% efficiency relative to a naive optimizer when an adaptive adversary employs a deceptive threat prior. All findings are validated using paired t-tests with Bonferroni correction and two-level variance decomposition, confirming that the performance gaps reported are structural properties of placement strategies rather than sampling artifacts.

</details>


### [166] [OrchestraBench: Evaluating Multi-Agent Orchestration Failure Modes, Recovery, and Decomposition Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05263)
*Yidian Chen,Yingzi Gu,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty,Sharon Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OrchestraBench提出了一种可控、可种子复现的故障注入基准测试框架，用于评估多智能体编排管道的故障、恢复与分解能力。它引入级联半径和按故障模式恢复率作为核心指标，并通过受控机制探针揭示了工具故障可完全恢复、模糊委派可部分恢复、潜在或语义故障不可恢复的三层级失效处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体编排框架正从演示走向生产，但现有基准通常只报告任务准确率，无法诊断pipeline失败的原因、级联从何处开始，或哪次路由决策导致了故障。缺乏对故障归因和中途恢复的可解释性评估。

Method: 通过模板化企业工作流上的可控、种子可复现故障注入机制，引入级联半径和按故障模式恢复率两个主要指标；使用bootstrap置信区间和配对检验比较路由策略；在26例金标准标注诊断上对比关键词/标志路由器与意图推理模型路由器；使用真实Claude agent通过可验证的算术依赖链进行受控机制探针，并跨Opus、Sonnet、Haiku模型及贷款审批工作流重构进行验证。

Result: 关键词/标志路由器在具有误导性或缺失表面标志的对抗性案例上得分0%，而意图推理模型路由器得分100%，与oracle持平。五类MAST模式下呈现三层失效处理能力：工具故障完全恢复(1.0)、模糊委派部分恢复(0.30)、三种潜在或语义模式完全不可恢复(0.0)。盲重试复现潜在故障并增加检测时间。级联半径随pipeline深度增加（深度3-7时均值从0.9增至4.7）。可信状态修复消融表明表观遏制收益主要来自可信状态信号而非自主检测。

Conclusion: 检测与归因是故障遏制的前提，盲重试无法作为有效策略；级联问题随深度加剧；路由器的质量对对抗性场景决定性关键。研究定位为受控链机制探针而非领域负载实验结果。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration frameworks are moving from demos to production, yet benchmarks typically report task accuracy without diagnosing why a pipeline failed, where a cascade began, or which routing decision caused the breakdown. OrchestraBench evaluates failure, recovery, and decomposition through a controlled, seed-reproducible failure-injection harness over templated enterprise workflows. It introduces cascade radius and per-failure-mode recovery as primary metrics and compares routing policies with bootstrap confidence intervals and paired tests. On a 26-case gold-labelled diagnostic, a keyword/flag router scored 0% on adversarial cases with misleading or missing surface flags, whereas an intent-reasoning model router scored 100%, matching the oracle. Controlled mechanism probes with a real Claude agent over a verifiable arithmetic dependency chain revealed three failure-handling tiers across five MAST modes: tool faults recovered fully (1.0), ambiguous delegation recovered partially (0.30), and three latent or semantic modes never recovered (0.0). This ordering persisted when the computation was reframed as a loan-approval workflow and across Sonnet, Opus, and Haiku, although absolute rates shifted with context. Blind retry reproduced latent faults and increased time to detection, indicating that detection and attribution are necessary for containment. Cascade radius increased with pipeline depth (mean 0.9 to 4.7 across depths 3-7). A trusted-state repair ablation showed that apparent containment gains primarily came from the trusted-state signal rather than autonomous detection. These results are controlled-chain mechanism probes, not domain-workload claims.

</details>


### [167] [Agentic self-driving microscopy benchmarks support qualification but do not necessarily generalize to unseen tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05266)
*Nathan S Johnson,Ian Abshire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个针对大型语言模型（LLM）智能体控制显微镜等科学表征工具的基准测试与追踪日志框架，系统评估了不同智能体架构（一、二、三智能体图拓扑）、五种LLM、RAG和上下文参数等配置在53项显微镜基准测试中的表现。共记录105种配置、1,949次测试运行和49,109次RAG检索。结果显示配置间在延迟、token使用、成本和失败模式上存在明显差异，但基于智能体架构训练的替代模型无法可靠预测其在未见任务上的表现，表明当前异构测试套件适用于资格认证、回归测试和诊断，但不支持任务无关的全局配置模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体正被越来越多地用于控制科学表征工具（如显微镜和同步辐射光束线），但智能体控制物理基础设施的研究尚处于起步阶段，缺乏成熟的工程范式。研究者需要理解不同智能体架构选择（LLM类型、智能体数量、职责分配、RAG参数等）如何影响任务性能，以及如何确保智能体能泛化到未见过的任务。

Method: 开发了一个基准测试和追踪日志框架，用于评估不同智能体架构配置在显微镜任务上的表现。框架涵盖一、二、三智能体图拓扑，五种LLM，RAG和上下文参数以及操作约束。通过53项显微镜基准测试，系统记录了105种配置、1,949次测试运行和49,109次RAG检索的数据，并使用替代模型（surrogate model）训练来测试其对未见任务表现的预测能力。

Result: 不同配置在延迟、token使用、成本和失败模式上存在显著差异；直接比较可清晰揭示这些差异。然而，基于智能体架构和测试结果训练的替代模型无法可靠预测智能体在新任务上的性能表现，表明当前异构测试套件不支持任务无关的全局配置模型。

Conclusion: 该基准测试框架对于智能体的资格认证（qualification）、回归测试、诊断和直接比较是有价值的工具，但当前的测试套件不足以支持建立一个可跨任务泛化的统一配置模型。这意味着在智能体控制物理基础设施的实际部署中，需要针对具体任务域进行配置优化，而不能依赖一个通用的最优配置。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly being developed to control a wide range of scientific characterization tools including microscopes and synchrotron beamlines. Research into agentic control of physical infrastructure is nascent and there are few well-established paradigms for how to engineer an agentic system. There are many choices to make when designing a microscopy agent, including the choice of LLM, the number of agents to use, agent responsibilities and delegation rules, retrieval-augmented generation parameters, and more. When designing and optimizing an agentic microscope controller, researchers not only want to ensure that the agent can correctly perform known tasks but also that the agent can generalize to new tasks that it has not encountered before. In this study, we develop a benchmark and trace-logging framework that reveals a) how different choices of agent architecture impact performance at microscopy tasks and b) the limitations of benchmarks for predicting if a particular agent will perform well on unseen microscopy tasks. The framework was used to evaluate one-, two-, and three-agent graph topologies, five LLMs, RAG and context parameters, and operational constraints across 53 microscopy benchmark tests. In total, 105 agent configurations, 1,949 individual test runs, and 49,109 RAG retrievals were recorded. Direct comparisons showed clear differences in latency, token use, cost, and failure mode between configurations. However, surrogate models trained on agent architecture and test results did not reliably predict an agent's performance on new, unseen tasks. These results show that these benchmarks are useful for qualification, regression testing, diagnosis, and direct comparison, but the current heterogeneous test suite does not support a task-independent global configuration model.
